    local retreat=state.hum.Health&lt;state.hum.MaxHealth*.33
    local preferred=retreat and 18 or state.style==1 and 9 or state.style==2 and 13 or 10.5
    if now&gt;state.burstUntil and state.rng:NextNumber()&lt;.025 then
        state.burstUntil=now+state.rng:NextNumber(.4,.9)
        state.strafeDirection=-state.strafeDirection
    end
    local radial=(distance&lt;preferred and -to*(preferred-distance+2) or to*math.min(3,distance-preferred))
    -- Strafing around the player is less robotic than direct MoveTo each tick.
    return state.root.Position+side*(now&lt;state.burstUntil and 17 or state.style==2 and 11 or 7)+radial
end

Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child.Name=="LashboundBattleArena" then task.defer(spawnAll,child) end
end)
local first=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LashboundBattleArena")
if first then task.defer(spawnAll,first) end
-- One scheduler for all six NPCs. No RunService-per-bot listeners.
while script.Parent do
    local now=os.clock()
    for i=#bots,1,-1 do
        local bot=bots[i]
        if bot.dead or not bot.model.Parent or not bot.arena.Parent or bot.hum.Health&lt;=0 then
            table.remove(bots,i)
        else
            animateGait(bot,now)
            preventSunkenSentinel(bot,now)
            local target,dist=findNearest(bot)
            if now&lt;(bot.model:GetAttribute("BotFrozenUntil") or 0) then
                bot.hum:MoveTo(bot.root.Position)
            elseif target and dist&lt;DISENGAGE_RADIUS then
                local dodged=npcDodge(bot,target,now,dist)
                if not dodged and now&gt;=(bot.model:GetAttribute("BotDodgeUntil") or 0) and not bot.pendingAttack then
                    local sight=hasSight(bot,target)
                    if sight then
                        bot.route=nil
                        if dist&lt;14 then
                            bot.hum:MoveTo(circlePosition(bot,target,dist,now))
                            if dist&lt;12.7 then attack(bot,target) end
                        elseif dist&lt;27 then
                            bot.hum:MoveTo(circlePosition(bot,target,dist,now))
                            -- Occasional jump/cut to avoid predictable loops.
                            if now&gt;bot.thinkingUntil and bot.rng:NextNumber()&lt;.13 then
                                bot.hum.Jump=true
                                bot.thinkingUntil=now+2.2
                            end
                        else
                            bot.hum:MoveTo(target.root.Position)
                        end
                    else
                        -- Pathfinding only when line of sight is obstructed; maximum every 3s.
                        if now-bot.lastPath&gt;3 then
                            bot.lastPath=now
                            getPath(bot,target)
                        end
                        local wp=bot.route and bot.route[bot.wp]
                        if wp then
                            if (bot.root.Position-wp.Position).Magnitude&lt;5 then
                                bot.wp+=1; wp=bot.route[bot.wp]
                            end
                            if wp then
                                if wp.Action==Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then bot.hum.Jump=true end
                                bot.hum:MoveTo(wp.Position)
                            else bot.hum:MoveTo(target.root.Position) end
                        else bot.hum:MoveTo(target.root.Position) end
                    end
                end
            else
                bot.route=nil
                bot.hum:MoveTo(bot.root.Position)
            end
        end
    end
    task.wait(.2)
end

        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V6 | ONE-WAY entrance. The battle arena contains NO exit door.
-- Death returns players to the safe lobby. Maps rotate independently.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local door=lobby and lobby:WaitForChild("ArenaDoorTrigger",15)
local request=RS:WaitForChild("LashboundPortalRequest",15)
if not door or not request then warn("[Lashbound Door] Entry parts missing") return end
local nextUse={}
local rng=Random.new()
local function inside(root,interact)
    local p=door.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
    local x=interact and 13 or 8
    local z=interact and 13 or 5.8
    return math.abs(p.X)&lt;x and math.abs(p.Y)&lt;12 and math.abs(p.Z)&lt;z
end
-- Pick a free-ish spawn instead of stacking newcomers on existing fighters.
-- Uses both current character positions and recent portal reservations so two
 -- simultaneous entrants do not get the same marker.
local recentSpawnReservations={}
local function destination()
    local arena=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LashboundBattleArena")
    local spawns=arena and arena:FindFirstChild("ArenaSpawns")
    if not spawns then return nil end
    local choices={}
    for _,part in ipairs(spawns:GetChildren()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(choices,part) end
    end
    if #choices==0 then return nil end
    local now=os.clock()
    local best=nil
    local bestScore=-math.huge
    for _,spot in ipairs(choices) do
        local nearest=250
        for _,other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if other:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena" then
                local c=other.Character
                local root=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then nearest=math.min(nearest,(root.Position-spot.Position).Magnitude) end
            end
        end
        local penalty=((recentSpawnReservations[spot] or 0)&gt;now) and 1000 or 0
        local score=nearest-penalty+rng:NextNumber(0,8)
        if score&gt;bestScore then bestScore=score;best=spot end
    end
    if not best then return nil end
    recentSpawnReservations[best]=now+4
    local p=best.Position+Vector3.new(0,3.5,0)
    local look=Vector3.new(0,p.Y,-620)
    if (look-p).Magnitude&lt;1 then look=p+Vector3.new(0,0,1) end
    return CFrame.lookAt(p,look)
end
local function enter(player,interact)
    if not player or player.Parent~=Players then return end
    if player:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena" then return end
    if os.clock()&lt;(nextUse[player] or 0) then return end
    local character=player.Character
    local hum=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or hum.Health&lt;=0 or not root or not inside(root,interact) then return end
    local cf=destination()
    if not cf then warn("[Lashbound Door] Arena still loading") return end
    nextUse[player]=os.clock()+2.5
    player:SetAttribute("Zone","Arena")
    player:SetAttribute("SafeUntil",os.clock()+3)
    character:PivotTo(cf)
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
    print("[Lashbound Door] Entered:",player.Name)
end
-- Backups for Touch, E-key prompt, mobile input and missed touch events.
door.CanCollide=false
door.CanTouch=true
local prompt=door:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
if not prompt then prompt=Instance.new("ProximityPrompt");prompt.Parent=door end
prompt.ActionText="Enter Battle"
prompt.ObjectText="ONE WAY: FIGHT UNTIL DEFEATED"
prompt.RequiresLineOfSight=false
prompt.HoldDuration=0
prompt.MaxActivationDistance=15
prompt.KeyboardKeyCode=Enum.KeyCode.F -- E remains reserved for Dodge.
prompt.Triggered:Connect(function(player)enter(player,true) end)
door.Touched:Connect(function(touched)
    local model=touched:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local player=model and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
    if player then enter(player,false) end
end)
request.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player,action)
    if action=="Enter" then enter(player,true) end
    -- "Exit" intentionally ignored: there is no arena escape.
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while script.Parent do
        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player:GetAttribute("Zone")~="Arena" then
                local c=player.Character
                local root=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and inside(root,false) then enter(player,false) end
            end
        end
        task.wait(.22)
    end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)nextUse[p]=nil end)
print("[Lashbound Door] One-way lobby-to-arena gateway ready")

-- Preserve arena terrain, which is centered 620 studs away.
local function clearCabinTerrain()
    Workspace.Terrain:FillBlock(CFrame.new(0,18,0),Vector3.new(174,76,174),Enum.Material.Air)
end

    <Item class="Script" referent="RBX03272">
      <Properties>
        <string name="Name">LashboundMapRotation</string>
        <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V6 | five-minute server-authoritative arena rotation.
-- Only ONE map is cloned into Workspace; unused map templates stay in ServerStorage.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local templates = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("LashboundMapTemplates",15)
if not templates then warn("[Lashbound Maps] Map templates are missing") return end
local order = {
    {key="PrismCitadel", name="PRISM CITADEL"},
    {key="CinderFoundry", name="CINDER FOUNDRY"},
    {key="GlacialDivide", name="GLACIAL DIVIDE"},
    {key="ThornTemple", name="THORN TEMPLE"},
    {key="StormReactor", name="STORM REACTOR"},
}
local DURATION = 300 -- Exactly 5 minutes, server-wide.
-- For a quick local test temporarily change DURATION to 25 and then back to 300.
local index = 0
local currentArena
local hazards={}
local nextDamage={}
local nextChange=0
local rng=Random.new()
local function findSpawnCf(arena)
    local group=arena and arena:FindFirstChild("ArenaSpawns")
    local spots=group and group:GetChildren() or {}
    if #spots&gt;0 then
        local marker=spots[rng:NextInteger(1,#spots)]
        if marker and marker:IsA("BasePart") then
            local p=marker.Position+Vector3.new(0,3.5,0)
            local center=Vector3.new(0,p.Y,-620)
            if (center-p).Magnitude&lt;1 then center=p+Vector3.new(0,0,1) end
            return CFrame.lookAt(p,center)
        end
    end
    return CFrame.new(0,6,-620)
end
-- Share spawn markers across all players instead of randomly piling fighters
-- on the same point when maps rotate during a crowded match.
local function relocateArenaPlayers(arena)
    local folder=arena and arena:FindFirstChild("ArenaSpawns")
    local points={}
    if folder then
        for _,p in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(points,p) end
        end
    end
    for i=#points,2,-1 do
        local j=rng:NextInteger(1,i)
        points[i],points[j]=points[j],points[i]
    end
    local count=0
    for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena" then
            local char=player.Character
            local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum and hum.Health&gt;0 and root then
                count+=1
                player:SetAttribute("SafeUntil",os.clock()+4)
                local cf
                if #points&gt;0 then
                    local spot=points[((count-1)%#points)+1]
                    cf=CFrame.new(spot.Position+Vector3.new(0,3.5,0))
                    local face=Vector3.new(0,cf.Position.Y,-620)
                    if (face-cf.Position).Magnitude&gt;1 then
                        cf=CFrame.lookAt(cf.Position,face)
                    end
                else
                    cf=findSpawnCf(arena)
                end
                char:PivotTo(cf)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
                hum.Health=hum.MaxHealth
            end
        end
    end
end
local function collectHazards(arena)
    hazards={}
    for _,part in ipairs(arena:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and string.sub(part.Name,1,7)=="Hazard_" then
            table.insert(hazards,part)
        end
    end
end
local function rotate()
    local nextIndex=(index%#order)+1
    local template=templates:FindFirstChild(order[nextIndex].key)
    if not template then warn("[Lashbound Maps] Missing map "..order[nextIndex].key) return end
    local replacement=template:Clone()
    replacement.Name="LashboundBattleArena"
    replacement:SetAttribute("BiomeIndex",nextIndex)
    -- Rename old first so scripts requesting the active map never pick it by mistake.
    local old=currentArena
    if old and old.Parent then old.Name="RetiringBattleArena" end
    replacement.Parent=Workspace
    currentArena=replacement
    index=nextIndex
    collectHazards(replacement)
    nextDamage={}
    nextChange=Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()+DURATION
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundMapName",order[index].name)
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundMapIndex",index)
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundNextMapTime",nextChange)
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundMapDuration",DURATION)
    relocateArenaPlayers(replacement)
    if old and old.Parent then old:Destroy() end
    print("[Lashbound Maps] Active:",order[index].name," next rotation in",DURATION,"seconds")
end

local function standingOnHazard(root,part)
    local p=part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
    return math.abs(p.X)&lt;part.Size.X/2+1.6
        and math.abs(p.Z)&lt;part.Size.Z/2+1.6
        and p.Y&gt;=-2 and p.Y&lt;=4.8
end
-- One low-frequency loop handles both the timer and map hazards.
-- No continuous per-part listeners or RenderStepped physics effects.
rotate()
while script.Parent do
    if Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()&gt;=nextChange then rotate() end
    if #hazards&gt;0 then
        local now=os.clock()
        local stormActive=(index==5) and (math.floor(Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())%8&lt;2)
        for _,part in ipairs(hazards) do
            if part.Parent and string.find(part.Name,"Surge") then
                part.Transparency=stormActive and .16 or .8
            end
        end
        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena" and now&gt;=(nextDamage[player] or 0)
               and now&gt;=(player:GetAttribute("SafeUntil") or 0)
               and now&gt;=(player:GetAttribute("DodgeUntil") or 0) then
                local character=player.Character
                local hum=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum and hum.Health&gt;0 and root then
                    for _,part in ipairs(hazards) do
                        if part.Parent and (not string.find(part.Name,"Surge") or stormActive)
                            and standingOnHazard(root,part) then
                            hum:TakeDamage(10)
                            nextDamage[player]=now+1.05
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    task.wait(.5)
end

                hum.Health=math.min(hum.MaxHealth,hum.Health+HEAL_PER_SECOND*.5)
                player:SetAttribute("Regenerating",true)
            else
                player:SetAttribute("Regenerating",false)
            end
        elseif player.Parent==Players then
            player:SetAttribute("Regenerating",false)
        end
    end

    <Item class="Script" referent="RBX03274">
      <Properties>

        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V11: at 1:50 show an incoming raid warning; at 2:00 spawn 30 hounds.

local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local ServerScriptService=game:GetService("ServerScriptService")
local award=ServerScriptService:WaitForChild("LashboundBotCoins")
local Feedback=RS:WaitForChild("LashboundV5Remotes"):WaitForChild("Feedback")

local RAID_AT=120
local WARN_AT=110
local DOG_COUNT=30
local TICK=.08
local CENTER=Vector3.new(0,0,-620)
local currentArena=nil
local epoch=0
local dogList={}
local aliveCount=0
local function announce(state,arenaIndex)
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidState",state)
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidMapIndex",arenaIndex or 0)
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidAlive",aliveCount)

announce("WAITING",0)
local function rootPart(model,name,size,color)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name=name;p.Size=size;p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CanQuery=false
    p.Material=Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic;p.Color=color;p.CastShadow=false;p.Parent=model
    return p
end
local function nearPlayer(pos)
    local best=math.huge
    local target=nil
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena" then
            local c=p.Character
            local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h and h.Health&gt;0 and r then
                local delta=r.Position-pos
                local d=Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z).Magnitude
                if d&lt;best then best=d;target={player=p,hum=h,root=r} end
            end
        end
    end
    return target,best
end
local function chooseSpawn(arena,folder,index)
    local params=RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
    params.RespectCanCollide=true
    -- Exclude the dog folder from the downcast by making all dog pieces non-queryable.
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances={arena}
    local fighters={}
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena" then
            local c=p.Character
            local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if r and h and h.Health&gt;0 then table.insert(fighters,r.Position) end
        end
    end
    local base=#fighters&gt;0 and fighters[(index-1)%#fighters+1] or CENTER
    for attempt=1,48 do
        local angle=RNG:NextNumber(0,math.pi*2)
        local radius=RNG:NextNumber(42,114)
        local x=math.clamp(base.X+math.cos(angle)*radius,-206,206)
        local z=math.clamp(base.Z+math.sin(angle)*radius,-826,-414)
        local hit=Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(x,120,z),Vector3.new(0,-150,0),params)
        if hit and hit.Normal.Y&gt;.65 and hit.Position.Y&lt;16
            and not string.find(hit.Instance.Name,"Hazard_")
            and not string.find(hit.Instance.Name,"Marker") then
            return Vector3.new(x,hit.Position.Y+1.92,z)
        end
    end
    return Vector3.new(RNG:NextNumber(-75,75),2.05,-620+RNG:NextNumber(-75,75))
end
local function createDog(arena,folder,index,createdEpoch)
    local pos=chooseSpawn(arena,folder,index)
    local model=Instance.new("Model")
    model.Name=string.format("RaidHound_%02d",index)
    model:SetAttribute("LashboundNPC",true) -- existing whip system can damage it
    model:SetAttribute("LashboundDog",true)
    local root=rootPart(model,"HumanoidRootPart",Vector3.new(1.8,1.9,2.8),Color3.new(0,0,0))
    root.Transparency=1
    local palette={Color3.fromRGB(70,57,59),Color3.fromRGB(78,79,89),Color3.fromRGB(97,69,53),Color3.fromRGB(47,50,56)}
    local fur=palette[(index-1)%#palette+1]
    local body=rootPart(model,"Body",Vector3.new(2.65,2.0,4.45),fur)
    body.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
    body.Material=Enum.Material.Fabric
    local head=rootPart(model,"Head",Vector3.new(2.0,1.7,1.9),fur)
    head.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
    head.Material=Enum.Material.Fabric
    local snout=rootPart(model,"Muzzle",Vector3.new(1.1,.65,1),Color3.fromRGB(193,173,138))
    local eyeL=rootPart(model,"EyeL",Vector3.new(.27,.27,.2),Color3.fromRGB(255,98,79))
    local eyeR=rootPart(model,"EyeR",Vector3.new(.27,.27,.2),Color3.fromRGB(255,98,79))
    eyeL.Material=Enum.Material.Neon;eyeR.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
    local legs={}
    for i=1,4 do legs[i]=rootPart(model,"Leg_"..i,Vector3.new(.65,1.35,.72),fur) end
    local tail=rootPart(model,"Tail",Vector3.new(.48,.5,1.65),fur)
    tail.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
    -- V25 animal silhouette: articulated-looking upper/lower legs + paws,
    -- upright ears, muzzle/black nose, chest/haunch fur, flexible tail tip.
    -- All ornaments are non-queryable and client-posed; the anchored invisible
    -- root alone remains the server-controlled health/attack target.
    local cosmetics={}
    local function animal(name,size,color,shape,mat)
        local p=rootPart(model,name,size,color)
        if shape then p.Shape=shape end
        p.Material=mat or Enum.Material.Fabric
        cosmetics[name]=p
        return p
    end
    animal("Ruff",Vector3.new(2.75,1.6,1.25),Color3.fromRGB(116,101,89),Enum.PartType.Ball)
    animal("Haunches",Vector3.new(2.5,1.7,1.45),fur,Enum.PartType.Ball)
    animal("Belly",Vector3.new(1.65,.75,2.55),Color3.fromRGB(114,100,86),Enum.PartType.Ball)
    animal("Left Ear",Vector3.new(.49,1.1,.52),fur,nil)
    animal("Right Ear",Vector3.new(.49,1.1,.52),fur,nil)
    animal("Nose",Vector3.new(.52,.31,.34),Color3.fromRGB(30,27,30),Enum.PartType.Ball,Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
    animal("Jaw",Vector3.new(1.06,.27,.82),Color3.fromRGB(113,85,73),nil)
    animal("Tongue",Vector3.new(.34,.11,.45),Color3.fromRGB(204,82,91),nil,Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
    animal("TailTip",Vector3.new(.47,.47,.94),fur,Enum.PartType.Ball)
    animal("Collar",Vector3.new(1.85,.29,1.4),Color3.fromRGB(98,44,41),nil,Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
    for i=1,4 do
        animal("LowerLeg_"..i,Vector3.new(.57,.85,.58),fur,nil)
        animal("Paw_"..i,Vector3.new(.72,.29,.93),Color3.fromRGB(62,54,53),nil,Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
    end
    model:SetAttribute("DogRigVersion",25)
    model:SetAttribute("DogRunning",false)
    local hum=Instance.new("Humanoid")
    hum.Name="Humanoid";hum.RequiresNeck=false;hum.BreakJointsOnDeath=false
    hum.MaxHealth=48;hum.Health=48;hum.WalkSpeed=0
    hum.DisplayDistanceType=Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
    hum.Parent=model
    model.PrimaryPart=root
    model.Parent=folder
    local offsets={
        [root]=CFrame.new(), [body]=CFrame.new(0,.12,0),[head]=CFrame.new(0,.43,-2.20),
        [snout]=CFrame.new(0,.03,-3.11),
        [eyeL]=CFrame.new(-.55,.76,-2.94),[eyeR]=CFrame.new(.55,.76,-2.94),
        [tail]=CFrame.new(0,.60,2.6)*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-19),0,0),
        [legs[1]]=CFrame.new(-.83,-1.14,-1.15),[legs[2]]=CFrame.new(.83,-1.14,-1.15),
        [legs[3]]=CFrame.new(-.83,-1.14,1.18),[legs[4]]=CFrame.new(.83,-1.14,1.18)
    }
    local state={model=model,hum=hum,root=root,pos=pos,dir=Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
        lastHit=-10,windup=false,epoch=createdEpoch,index=index,speed=RNG:NextNumber(14,19),
        wobble=RNG:NextNumber(0,7),alive=true,style=RNG:NextNumber(.4,1.5)}
    local health=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    health.Name="DogHealth";health.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(110,40)
    health.StudsOffsetWorldSpace=Vector3.new(0,2.1,0)
    health.AlwaysOnTop=true;health.MaxDistance=85;health.Parent=head
    local lbl=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,.65);lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1;lbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,176,130)
    lbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold;lbl.TextScaled=true;lbl.TextStrokeTransparency=.1;lbl.Parent=health
    local track=Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Position=UDim2.fromScale(.08,.71);track.Size=UDim2.fromScale(.84,.18)
    track.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(42,21,29);track.BorderSizePixel=0;track.Parent=health
    local fill=Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1);fill.BorderSizePixel=0;fill.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(252,128,92);fill.Parent=track
    hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(n)
        if lbl.Parent then lbl.Text="RAID HOUND  "..math.ceil(math.max(0,n)) end
        if fill.Parent then fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(n/hum.MaxHealth,0,1),1) end
    end)
    lbl.Text="RAID HOUND 48"
    local initial=CFrame.lookAt(pos,pos+state.dir)
    for obj,offset in pairs(offsets) do obj.CFrame=initial*offset end
    -- Initialize every extra part at its actual local pose: this avoids a
    -- one-frame spawn flash at the map origin while the client rig loads.
    local init={
        Ruff=CFrame.new(0,.20,-1.35),Haunches=CFrame.new(0,.12,1.4),
        Belly=CFrame.new(0,-.69,.10),["Left Ear"]=CFrame.new(-.68,1.49,-2.22),
        ["Right Ear"]=CFrame.new(.68,1.49,-2.22),
        Nose=CFrame.new(0,.17,-3.62),Jaw=CFrame.new(0,-.23,-3.0),
        Tongue=CFrame.new(0,-.4,-3.13),TailTip=CFrame.new(0,.77,3.52),
        Collar=CFrame.new(0,.35,-1.86),
    }
    for i=1,4 do
        local x=(i%2==1) and -.83 or .83
        local z=i&lt;3 and -1.16 or 1.2
        init["LowerLeg_"..i]=CFrame.new(x,-1.47,z)
        init["Paw_"..i]=CFrame.new(x,-1.76,z-.19)
    end
    for name,offset in pairs(init) do
        local piece=cosmetics[name]
        if piece then piece.CFrame=initial*offset end
    end
    -- Server updates the invisible collision/health root only. Clients animate
    -- the individual paws and body each frame using root's replicated CFrame.
    state.draw=function()
        if state.alive and root.Parent then
            local lifted=Vector3.zero
            local startAt=state.model:GetAttribute("JudgementLiftStart") or 0
            local untilAt=state.model:GetAttribute("JudgementLiftUntil") or 0
            if startAt&gt;0 and os.clock()&lt;untilAt then
                local phase=math.clamp((os.clock()-startAt)/math.max(.1,untilAt-startAt),0,1)
                lifted=Vector3.new(0,math.sin(phase*math.pi)*14,0)
            end
            local place=state.pos+lifted
            root.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(place,place+state.dir)
        end
    end
    hum.Died:Connect(function()
        if not state.alive then return end
        state.alive=false
        if state.epoch==epoch and arena==currentArena then
            aliveCount=math.max(0,aliveCount-1)
            RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidAlive",aliveCount)
            local killerId=hum:GetAttribute("LastAttackerId")
            local killer=killerId and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(killerId)
            if killer and killer:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena"
                and os.clock()-(hum:GetAttribute("PlayerHitAt") or -100)&gt;-1
                and os.clock()-(hum:GetAttribute("PlayerHitAt") or -100)&lt;12 then
                award:Fire(killer,1) -- exactly ONE coin, uses persistent wallet
                Feedback:FireClient(killer,"Message","RAID HOUND DEFEATED! +1 COIN")
            end
            if aliveCount==0 then announce("CLEARED",RS:GetAttribute("LashboundMapIndex")) end
        end
        task.delay(.6,function()if model.Parent then model:Destroy() end end)
    end)
    table.insert(dogList,state)
end
local function startRaid(arena,mapIndex,myEpoch)
    if arena~=currentArena or epoch~=myEpoch then return end
    local folder=arena:FindFirstChild("LashboundBots") or Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name="LashboundBots";folder.Parent=arena
    aliveCount=0
    for i=1,DOG_COUNT do
        if epoch~=myEpoch or arena~=currentArena then return end
        -- A single malformed spawn should not silently cancel the other hounds.
        local ok,err=pcall(createDog,arena,folder,i,myEpoch)
        if ok then
            aliveCount+=1
        else
            warn("[Lashbound Hounds] Spawn failed",i,err)
        end
        RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidSpawned",i)
        RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidAlive",aliveCount)
        if i%6==0 then task.wait(.04) end
    end
    announce("ACTIVE",mapIndex)
    print("[Lashbound Raid] dogs spawned:",aliveCount,"out of",DOG_COUNT,"map",mapIndex)
end
local function reset(arena)
    epoch+=1
    currentArena=arena
    dogList={}
    aliveCount=0
    RS:SetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidSpawned",0)
    announce("WAITING",RS:GetAttribute("LashboundMapIndex") or 0)
end
local function stepDog(state,dt,now)
    if not state.alive or not state.model.Parent or not state.root.Parent then return end
    local status=state.model:GetAttribute("LashboundStatus")
    if now&lt;(state.model:GetAttribute("BotFrozenUntil") or 0)
        or status=="Freeze" or status=="Shock" then
        state.draw(now)
        return
    end
    local target,distance=nearPlayer(state.pos)
    local direction=state.dir
    -- Sentinel-style awareness: patrol when players are distant, chase when near.
    if distance&gt;125 then target=nil end
    if not target then
        local wanderAt=state.wanderAt or 0
        if now&gt;=wanderAt then
            local angle=now*.17+state.index*2.399
            state.patrol=Vector3.new(math.cos(angle),0,math.sin(angle))
            state.wanderAt=now+3+(state.index%4)
        end
        direction=state.patrol or direction
    end
    if target then
        local delta=target.root.Position-state.pos
        local flat=Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude&gt;1 then direction=flat.Unit end
        -- In combat these hounds circle and burst forward rather than simply beeline.
        if distance&lt;14 then
            local side=Vector3.new(-direction.Z,0,direction.X)
            direction=(direction*.45+side*math.sin(now*1.6+state.index)*.72).Unit
        end
        local speed=state.speed * (distance&lt;6 and .35 or 1) * (status=="Slow" and .52 or 1)
        local step=direction*math.min(speed*dt,math.max(.0,distance-3.9))
        if step.Magnitude&gt;.01 then
            local params=RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local excluded={state.model,state.model.Parent}
            for _,otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if otherPlayer.Character then table.insert(excluded,otherPlayer.Character) end
            end
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances=excluded
            params.RespectCanCollide=true
            -- Swept collision prevents hounds running through walls/rock geometry.
            local hit=Workspace:Blockcast(CFrame.new(state.pos),Vector3.new(2,2,2.8),step,params)
            if hit and hit.Distance &lt; step.Magnitude then
                local tangent=Vector3.new(-direction.Z,0,direction.X)
                step=tangent*math.min(2.8,speed*dt)
                if Workspace:Blockcast(CFrame.new(state.pos),Vector3.new(2,2,2.8),step,params) then step=Vector3.zero end
            end
            local nextPos=state.pos+step
            -- Ground-check every tick, even at rest. Do not let dogs stand on
            -- the tops of tall props, avatar heads, or fall back to their old
            -- high Y position. Characters and hound body parts are excluded.
            local ground=Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(nextPos.X,18,nextPos.Z),Vector3.new(0,-48,0),params)
            if ground and ground.Normal.Y&gt;.60 and ground.Position.Y&lt;11
                and not string.find(ground.Instance.Name,"Hazard_") then
                local desiredY=ground.Position.Y+1.92
                local corrected=math.abs(desiredY-nextPos.Y)&gt;4 and desiredY or nextPos.Y+(desiredY-nextPos.Y)*math.min(1,dt*18)
                nextPos=Vector3.new(nextPos.X,corrected,nextPos.Z)
            else
                nextPos=state.pos -- stay on a valid surface
            end
            state.pos=nextPos
        end
        if distance&lt;7 and not state.windup and now-state.lastHit&gt;RNG:NextNumber(1.3,2.2) then
            state.windup=true
            state.model:SetAttribute("DogBiteAt",Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
            state.lastHit=now
            local attacker=target
            local token=state.epoch
            task.delay(.40,function()
                state.windup=false
                if not state.alive or token~=epoch or not state.model.Parent then return end
                local player=attacker.player
                if player:GetAttribute("Zone")~="Arena" or not attacker.root.Parent or attacker.hum.Health&lt;=0 then return end
                if (attacker.root.Position-state.pos).Magnitude&gt;8 then return end
                if os.clock()&lt;(player:GetAttribute("DodgeUntil") or 0)
                    or os.clock()&lt;(player:GetAttribute("SafeUntil") or 0) then return end
                local damage=6
                attacker.hum:TakeDamage(damage)
                player:SetAttribute("LastCombatAt",os.clock())
                Feedback:FireClient(player,"Message","HOUND BITE -6")
            end)
        end
    else
        -- Idle patrol uses a lightweight swept obstacle check, not 30 pathfinders.
        local step=direction*state.speed*.27*dt
        local params=RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances={state.model,state.model.Parent}
        params.RespectCanCollide=true
        local nextPos=state.pos+step
        local blocked=Workspace:Blockcast(CFrame.new(state.pos),Vector3.new(2,2,2.8),step,params)
        local ground=Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(nextPos.X,18,nextPos.Z),Vector3.new(0,-48,0),params)
        if blocked or not ground or ground.Normal.Y&lt;.65 or ground.Position.Y&gt;11
            or math.abs(nextPos.X)&gt;205 or math.abs(nextPos.Z+620)&gt;205 then
            state.patrol=-direction
            state.wanderAt=now+1.4
        else
            state.pos=Vector3.new(nextPos.X,ground.Position.Y+1.92,nextPos.Z)
        end
    end
    -- Recheck ground even without an aggro target; no suspended idle hounds.
    if currentArena and (state.pos.Y&gt;3 or math.floor(now*5+state.index)%3==0) then
        local p=RaycastParams.new()
        p.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
        p.FilterDescendantsInstances={currentArena}
        p.RespectCanCollide=true
        local hit=Workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(state.pos.X,18,state.pos.Z),Vector3.new(0,-48,0),p)
        if hit and hit.Normal.Y&gt;.6 and hit.Position.Y&lt;11 then
            state.pos=Vector3.new(state.pos.X,hit.Position.Y+1.92,state.pos.Z)
        end
    end
    state.dir=direction
    state.draw(now)
end
local sweep=0
while script.Parent do
    task.wait(TICK)
    sweep+=1
    local arena=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LashboundBattleArena")
    if arena~=currentArena then reset(arena) end
    if arena then
        local nextTime=RS:GetAttribute("LashboundNextMapTime")
        local dur=RS:GetAttribute("LashboundMapDuration")
        local index=RS:GetAttribute("LashboundMapIndex") or 0
        if type(nextTime)=="number" and type(dur)=="number" then
            local elapsed=math.max(0,Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()-(nextTime-dur))
            local state=RS:GetAttribute("LashboundDogRaidState")
            if elapsed&gt;=WARN_AT and elapsed&lt;RAID_AT and state=="WAITING" then
                announce("WARNING",index)
            elseif elapsed&gt;=RAID_AT and (state=="WAITING" or state=="WARNING") then
                announce("SPAWNING",index)
                local myEpoch=epoch
                task.spawn(startRaid,arena,index,myEpoch)
            end
        end
        for i=#dogList,1,-1 do
            local dog=dogList[i]
            if not dog.alive or not dog.model.Parent then
                table.remove(dogList,i)
            elseif i%2==sweep%2 then
                -- Each hound updates 5x/sec on the server, locally interpolated at 30fps.
                stepDog(dog,TICK*2,os.clock())
            end
        end
    end
end
</ProtectedString>
      </Properties>
    </Item>
    <Item class="Script" referent="RBX03275">
      <Properties>
        <string name="Name">LashboundOwnerService</string>
        <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V13.1: PERMANENT founder access for explicitly approved Roblox user IDs.
-- This server-side whitelist is bundled with the published place. It survives deaths,
-- reconnects, and DataStore failures, without adding the whip to public shop inventory.
-- The user who personally owns the experience also retains founder access.
local PERMANENT_FOUNDER_USER_IDS = {
    [3122892445] = true, -- explicitly authorized permanent Founder's Tempest holder
}
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerStorage=game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local Action=RS:WaitForChild("LashboundV5Remotes"):WaitForChild("Action")
local Config=require(RS:WaitForChild("WhipConfig"))
local ownerStates={}
local function verified(player)
    local id=player.UserId
    return PERMANENT_FOUNDER_USER_IDS[id]==true
        or (game.CreatorType==Enum.CreatorType.User and game.CreatorId&gt;0 and id==game.CreatorId)
end
local function getRoot(player)
    local ch=player.Character
    local hum=ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root=ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not hum or hum.Health&lt;=0 then return nil end
    return ch,hum,root
end
local function stopFlight(player)
    local state=ownerStates[player]
    if state then
        state.flying=false
        if state.constraint then state.constraint:Destroy();state.constraint=nil end
        if state.attachment then state.attachment:Destroy();state.attachment=nil end
        state.vector=Vector3.zero
        state.lastInput=0
    end
    local _,hum,root=getRoot(player)
    if hum then hum.PlatformStand=false;hum.AutoRotate=true end
    if root then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
        pcall(function() root:SetNetworkOwnershipAuto() end)
    end
    player:SetAttribute("OwnerFlying",false)
end
-- FounderTempest is kept in ServerStorage, never in StarterPack. Cloning it
-- into one verified player's Backpack preserves the ordinary LashboundWhip.
local boundTools=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local function bindFounderTool(player,tool)
    if boundTools[tool] then return end
    boundTools[tool]=true
    tool:SetAttribute("WhipId","Owner")
    tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
        if not verified(player) or not player.Character or tool.Parent~=player.Character then return end
        player:SetAttribute("OwnerEquipped",true)
        player:SetAttribute("SelectedWhip","Owner")
        player:SetAttribute("HasWhip",true)
    end)
    tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
        stopFlight(player)
        player:SetAttribute("OwnerEquipped",false)
        task.defer(function()
            if player.Parent and not (player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest")) then
                local regular=player:GetAttribute("RegularWhip") or "Rope"
                player:SetAttribute("SelectedWhip",regular)
                player:SetAttribute("HasWhip",player.Character~=nil and player.Character:FindFirstChild("LashboundWhip")~=nil)
            end
        end)
    end)
end
local function grantFounderTool(player)
    if not verified(player) then return end
    local ch=player.Character
    local hum=ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health&lt;=0 then return end
    local backpack=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if not backpack then return end
    local tool=ch:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest") or backpack:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest")
    if not tool then
        local template=ServerStorage:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest")
        if not template then warn("[Lashbound] FounderTempest template missing");return end
        tool=template:Clone()
        tool.Name="FounderTempest"
        tool.Parent=backpack
    end
    bindFounderTool(player,tool)
end
local function enableFlight(player)
    local state=ownerStates[player]
    local ch,hum,root=getRoot(player)
    if not state or not ch then return end
    stopFlight(player)
    local att=Instance.new("Attachment")
    att.Name="FounderFlightAttachment"
    att.Parent=root
    local mover=Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
    mover.Name="FounderFlightVelocity"
    mover.Attachment0=att
    mover.RelativeTo=Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
    mover.VelocityConstraintMode=Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Vector
    mover.ForceLimitsEnabled=false
    mover.VectorVelocity=Vector3.zero
    mover.Parent=root
    -- Server-owned flight prevents clients from bypassing arena collision limits.
    pcall(function()root:SetNetworkOwner(nil)end)
    hum.PlatformStand=true
    hum.AutoRotate=false
    state.flying=true
    state.attachment=att
    state.constraint=mover
    state.vector=Vector3.zero
    state.lastInput=os.clock()
    player:SetAttribute("OwnerFlying",true)
end
local function setUp(player)
    if not verified(player) then
        player:SetAttribute("OwnerVerified",false)
        return
    end
    ownerStates[player]={flying=false,vector=Vector3.zero,lastInput=0}
    player:SetAttribute("OwnerVerified",true)
    player:SetAttribute("OwnerFlying",false)
    player:SetAttribute("OwnerEquipped",false)
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
        stopFlight(player)
        -- Never force FounderTempest equipped or overwrite the normal loadout.
        task.delay(.7,function() if player.Character==ch and player.Parent then grantFounderTool(player) end end)
        task.delay(1.8,function() if player.Character==ch and player.Parent then grantFounderTool(player) end end)
    end)
    if player.Character then task.defer(grantFounderTool,player) end
end
Action.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player,action,param)
    if not verified(player) or not ownerStates[player] then return end
    if action=="OwnerFlyToggle" then
        if not (player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest"))
            or player:GetAttribute("OwnerEquipped")~=true then return end
        local state=ownerStates[player]
        if state.flying then stopFlight(player) else enableFlight(player) end
    elseif action=="OwnerFlyInput" then
        local state=ownerStates[player]
        if not state.flying or typeof(param)~="Vector3" then return end
        if param.X~=param.X or param.Y~=param.Y or param.Z~=param.Z or param.Magnitude&gt;1.05 then return end
        state.vector=param
        state.lastInput=os.clock()
    end
end)
-- 10Hz movement / boundary sweeps; no expensive per-frame remote updates.
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(.1) do
        for player,state in pairs(ownerStates) do
            if state.flying then
                local ch,hum,root=getRoot(player)
                if not ch or not state.constraint or not state.constraint.Parent then
                    stopFlight(player)
                else
                    local v=(os.clock()-state.lastInput&lt;.45) and state.vector or Vector3.zero
                    if v.Magnitude&gt;1 then v=v.Unit end
                    local desired=v*64
                    local pos=root.Position
                    local inArena=player:GetAttribute("Zone")=="Arena"
                    local centerZ=inArena and -620 or 0
                    local limit=inArena and 214 or 85
                    local nextPoint=pos+desired*.12
                    if math.abs(nextPoint.X)&gt;limit or math.abs(nextPoint.Z-centerZ)&gt;limit
                        or nextPoint.Y&gt;110 or nextPoint.Y&lt;4.5 then
                        desired=Vector3.zero
                    end
                    if desired.Magnitude&gt;.1 then
                        local params=RaycastParams.new()
                        params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                        params.FilterDescendantsInstances={ch}
                        params.RespectCanCollide=true
                        local hit=Workspace:Blockcast(CFrame.new(pos),Vector3.new(2.7,4.2,2.7),desired.Unit*math.max(5,desired.Magnitude*.14),params)
                        if hit then desired=Vector3.zero end
                    end
                    state.constraint.VectorVelocity=desired
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(setUp)
for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do task.defer(setUp,p) end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)stopFlight(p);ownerStates[p]=nil end)
</ProtectedString>
      </Properties>
    </Item>

local save -- forward-declared to support debounced saves triggered by rewards
local function queueSave(player)
    local d=data[player]
    if not d then return end
    d.Revision=(d.Revision or 0)+1
    d.Dirty=true
    player:SetAttribute("WalletSaveStatus",d.SaveDisabled and "OFFLINE" or "PENDING")
    if d.SaveQueued then return end
    d.SaveQueued=true
    task.delay(18,function()
        if data[player]~=d then return end
        d.SaveQueued=false
        if d.Dirty and d.Loaded and not d.SaveDisabled and save then save(player) end
    end)
end

    return DataStoreService:GetDataStore(RunService:IsStudio() and "LashboundArena_UserProgress_STUDIO_V9" or "LashboundArena_UserProgress_v1")
end)

local function notify(player,kind,msg,payload)
    if player.Parent == Players then Feedback:FireClient(player,kind,msg,payload) end
end
local function charInfo(player)
    local character = player.Character
    if not character then return nil end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or humanoid.Health&lt;=0 or not root then return nil end
    return character,humanoid,root
end
local function isArena(player)
    return player:GetAttribute("Zone") == "Arena"
end
local function clearWeaponStyle(tool)
    for _,obj in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
        if obj.Name:match("^Style_") then
            obj:Destroy()
        end
    end
end
local function styleWeld(part0, part1)
    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    weld.Name = "Style_Attached"
    weld.Part0 = part0
    weld.Part1 = part1
    weld.Parent = part1
end
local function stylePart(tool, parentPart, name, size, cf, color, material, shape, trans)
    local p = Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name = "Style_" .. name
    p.Size = size
    p.CFrame = parentPart.CFrame * cf
    p.Color = color
    p.Material = material or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    p.Transparency = trans or 0
    p.CanCollide = false
    p.CanTouch = false
    p.CanQuery = false
    p.CastShadow = false
    p.Massless = true
    if shape then p.Shape = shape end
    p.Parent = tool
    styleWeld(parentPart, p)
    return p
end

local function paintWeapon(tool, id)
    if not tool then return end
    local cfg = Whips[id] or Whips.Rope
    tool:SetAttribute("WhipId", id)
    tool.ToolTip = cfg.Name .. " • Q: " .. cfg.PowerName
    for _,piece in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
        if piece:IsA("BasePart") then
            local lashIndex = tonumber(piece.Name:match("Lash_(%d+)"))
            if piece.Name:match("^Lash_") or piece.Name=="LashTip" or piece.Name=="ColorCollar" then
                piece.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                piece.Transparency = 0
                piece.Color = cfg.Color
                if id=="Ember" then
                    if lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 or piece.Name=="LashTip" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,175,71); piece.Material=Enum.Material.Neon else piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(119,38,22) end
                elseif id=="Frost" then
                    piece.Color = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 and Color3.fromRGB(238,252,255) or Color3.fromRGB(148,238,255)
                    piece.Material = Enum.Material.Glass
                    piece.Transparency = piece.Name=="LashTip" and .08 or .18
                elseif id=="Volt" then
                    if lashIndex and lashIndex % 2 == 0 then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(104,244,255) else piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,241,99) end
                    piece.Material = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.Metal
                elseif id=="Gravity" then
                    piece.Color = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 and Color3.fromRGB(214,180,255) or Color3.fromRGB(76,59,120)
                    piece.Material = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 4 and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                elseif id=="Phantom" then
                    piece.Color = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 and Color3.fromRGB(255,176,244) or Color3.fromRGB(206,97,255)
                    piece.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                    piece.Transparency = piece.Name=="LashTip" and .04 or .14
                elseif id=="Echo" then
                    piece.Color=lashIndex and lashIndex&gt;=5 and Color3.fromRGB(168,255,244) or Color3.fromRGB(69,162,159)
                    piece.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
                    piece.Transparency=piece.Name=="LashTip" and .05 or .13
                elseif id=="Judgement" then
                    piece.Color=lashIndex and lashIndex%2==0 and Color3.fromRGB(115,231,255) or Color3.fromRGB(250,242,102)
                    piece.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
                elseif id=="Legend" then
                    piece.Color = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 and Color3.fromRGB(255,240,159) or Color3.fromRGB(255,196,82)
                    piece.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                elseif id=="Owner" then
                    piece.Color = lashIndex and lashIndex &gt;= 5 and Color3.fromRGB(255,246,203) or Color3.fromRGB(255,228,138)
                    piece.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                elseif id=="Rope" then
                    if lashIndex then piece.Color = lashIndex&gt;=5 and Color3.fromRGB(210,180,108) or Color3.fromRGB(123,92,61) end
                end
            elseif piece.Name:match("^GripWrap_") then
                piece.Color = (id=="Rope" and Color3.fromRGB(130,94,65)) or (id=="Ember" and Color3.fromRGB(111,42,28)) or (id=="Frost" and Color3.fromRGB(174,230,238)) or (id=="Volt" and Color3.fromRGB(65,98,112)) or (id=="Gravity" and Color3.fromRGB(78,69,120)) or (id=="Phantom" and Color3.fromRGB(94,63,112)) or Color3.fromRGB(145,111,73)
            elseif piece.Name=="Pommel" or piece.Name=="Handle" then
                if id=="Rope" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(73,54,40)
                elseif id=="Ember" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(68,35,28)
                elseif id=="Frost" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(159,194,205)
                elseif id=="Volt" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(78,86,105)
                elseif id=="Gravity" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(54,47,73)
                elseif id=="Phantom" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(67,46,94)
                elseif id=="Echo" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(49,96,93)
                elseif id=="Judgement" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(104,114,135)
                elseif id=="Legend" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(150,103,50)
                elseif id=="Owner" then piece.Color=Color3.fromRGB(181,155,86) end
            end
        end
    end
    styleWeapon(tool,id)
end
local function createEmergencyWeapon()
    -- Backup only. A real physical Tool is also serialized in StarterPack.
    local tool = Instance.new("Tool")
    tool.Name = "LashboundWhip"
    tool.CanBeDropped=false
    tool.RequiresHandle=true
    tool.Grip = CFrame.new(0,-.6,0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-12),0,math.rad(-9))
    local handle = Instance.new("Part")
    handle.Name="Handle"
    handle.Size=Vector3.new(.46,1.8,.46)
    handle.Color=Color3.fromRGB(48,49,67)
    handle.Material=Enum.Material.Metal
    handle.CanCollide=false; handle.CanTouch=false; handle.CanQuery=false
    handle.Massless=true
    handle.Parent=tool
    local pivot=Instance.new("Part")
    pivot.Name="LashPivot"; pivot.Size=Vector3.new(.18,.18,.18)
    pivot.Transparency=1; pivot.CanCollide=false; pivot.CanTouch=false; pivot.CanQuery=false; pivot.Massless=true
    pivot.CFrame=handle.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,.95,0)
    pivot.Parent=tool
    local joint=Instance.new("Motor6D")
    joint.Name="WhipJoint"; joint.Part0=handle; joint.Part1=pivot
    joint.C0=CFrame.new(0,.95,0); joint.Parent=handle
    for i=0,4 do
        local wrap = Instance.new("Part")
        wrap.Name = "GripWrap_"..i
        wrap.Size = Vector3.new(.53,.085,.53)
        wrap.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
        wrap.Color = Color3.fromRGB(134,101,79)
        wrap.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        wrap.CFrame = handle.CFrame * CFrame.new(0,-.63+.25*i,0)
        wrap.CanCollide=false; wrap.CanTouch=false; wrap.CanQuery=false; wrap.Massless=true
        wrap.Parent=tool
        styleWeld(handle,wrap)
    end
    local collar=Instance.new("Part")
    collar.Name="ColorCollar"; collar.Size=Vector3.new(.64,.20,.64); collar.Shape=Enum.PartType.Cylinder
    collar.Color=Color3.fromRGB(240,212,122); collar.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
    collar.CFrame=handle.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,.85,0)
    collar.CanCollide=false; collar.CanTouch=false; collar.CanQuery=false; collar.Massless=true
    collar.Parent=tool; styleWeld(handle,collar)
    local pommel=Instance.new("Part")
    pommel.Name="Pommel"; pommel.Size=Vector3.new(.66,.46,.66); pommel.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
    pommel.Color=Color3.fromRGB(156,127,94); pommel.Material=Enum.Material.Metal
    pommel.CFrame=handle.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,-.95,0)
    pommel.CanCollide=false; pommel.CanTouch=false; pommel.CanQuery=false; pommel.Massless=true
    pommel.Parent=tool; styleWeld(handle,pommel)
    local path={Vector3.new(0,0,0),Vector3.new(0,.45,-.65),Vector3.new(0,.9,-1.5),Vector3.new(0,1.15,-2.5),Vector3.new(0,1.22,-3.5),Vector3.new(0,1,-4.55),Vector3.new(0,.66,-5.55),Vector3.new(0,.24,-6.3)}
    for i=1,#path-1 do
        local a,b=path[i],path[i+1]
        local p=Instance.new("Part")
        p.Name="Lash_"..i
        p.Size=Vector3.new(math.max(.095,.36-i*.035),math.max(.095,.36-i*.035),(b-a).Magnitude*1.08)
        p.CFrame=pivot.CFrame*CFrame.lookAt((a+b)/2,b)
        p.Color=Whips.Rope.Color
        p.Material=(i&gt;=6) and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
        p.CanCollide=false; p.CanTouch=false; p.CanQuery=false; p.Massless=true
        p.Parent=tool
        local weld=Instance.new("WeldConstraint"); weld.Part0=pivot; weld.Part1=p; weld.Parent=p
    end
    local tip=Instance.new("Part")
    tip.Name="LashTip"; tip.Size=Vector3.new(.19,.19,.46)
    tip.Color=Color3.fromRGB(245,246,255); tip.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
    tip.CFrame=pivot.CFrame*CFrame.new(0,1.19,-6.42)
    tip.CanCollide=false; tip.CanTouch=false; tip.CanQuery=false; tip.Massless=true
    tip.Parent=tool; styleWeld(pivot,tip)
    paintWeapon(tool,"Rope")
    return tool
end

local function findWeapon(player)
    local char=player.Character
    local backpack=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    return (char and char:FindFirstChild("LashboundWhip")) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("LashboundWhip"))
end

local function attachCombatHealth(model,hum,display,color)
    local head=model:FindFirstChild("Head") or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not head or head:FindFirstChild("LashboundHealthBar") then return end
    local gui=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    gui.Name="LashboundHealthBar"
    gui.AlwaysOnTop=true;gui.MaxDistance=145
    gui.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(155,41)
    gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace=Vector3.new(0,3.1,0)
    gui.Parent=head
    local bg=Instance.new("Frame")
    bg.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,15);bg.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,23)
    bg.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(19,19,29);bg.BorderSizePixel=0;bg.Parent=gui
    local fill=Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Name="Fill";fill.BackgroundColor3=color or Color3.fromRGB(90,245,158)
    fill.BorderSizePixel=0;fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1);fill.Parent=bg
    local label=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,23);label.BackgroundTransparency=1
    label.TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1);label.TextStrokeTransparency=.2
    label.TextScaled=true;label.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent=gui
    local function update(hp)
        fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(hp/math.max(1,hum.MaxHealth),0,1),1)
        label.Text=display.."  "..math.ceil(hp).."/"..math.ceil(hum.MaxHealth)
    end
    hum.HealthChanged:Connect(update)
    update(hum.Health)
end

local function equipWeapon(player,forceNormal)
    local char,hum=charInfo(player)
    if not char then return end
    local bag=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if not bag then return end
    local tool=findWeapon(player)
    if not tool then
        tool=createEmergencyWeapon()
        tool.Parent=bag
    end
    -- A founder has TWO separate Tools. Never repaint the ordinary LashboundWhip
    -- as the owner item, and never steal the hotbar selection from FounderTempest.
    if char:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest") and not forceNormal then
        player:SetAttribute("HasWhip",true)
        return
    end
    local regularId=player:GetAttribute("RegularWhip") or "Rope"
    if not Whips[regularId] or Whips[regularId].OwnerOnly then regularId="Rope" end
    paintWeapon(tool,regularId)
    if tool.Parent~=char then hum:EquipTool(tool) end
    if tool.Parent==char then player:SetAttribute("SelectedWhip",regularId) end
    player:SetAttribute("HasWhip", tool.Parent==char)
    notify(player,"Message", tool.Parent==char and "WHIP EQUIPPED" or "WHIP IN BACKPACK - SELECT SLOT 1")
end
local function addCoins(player,amount)
    local d=data[player]
    if not d then return end
    d.Coins=math.max(0,math.floor(d.Coins+amount))
    player:SetAttribute("Coins",d.Coins)
    queueSave(player)
end
botCoinEvent.Event:Connect(function(player,amount)
    -- Server-only BindableEvent, never a client RemoteEvent.
    if player and player.Parent==Players and isArena(player) and (amount==10 or amount==1) then
        addCoins(player,amount)
        if amount==10 then notify(player,"Message","PICKED UP +10 COINS") end
    end
end)
bossCoinEvent.Event:Connect(function(player,amount)
    if player and player.Parent==Players and amount==120 and isArena(player) then
        addCoins(player,amount)
    end
end)
local function setKO(player)
    local d=data[player]
    if not d then return end
    d.KOs+=1
    player:SetAttribute("KOs",d.KOs)
    queueSave(player)
    addCoins(player,Config.PlayerKOReward)
    notify(player,"Message","PLAYER K.O.! +"..Config.PlayerKOReward.." coins")
end
-- Portal transfers are exclusively handled by PortalService; no Return combat action.
-- Low-overhead visuals: one temporary effect per attack, no physics/projectiles/AI loop.
local fxFolder=Instance.new("Folder")
fxFolder.Name="LightweightFX"
fxFolder.Parent=Workspace
local function fxLine(from,to,color,width)
    local delta=to-from
    if delta.Magnitude&lt;.2 then return end
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name="WhipFlash"; p.Anchored=true; p.CanCollide=false; p.CanTouch=false; p.CanQuery=false
    p.CastShadow=false; p.Material=Enum.Material.Neon; p.Color=color
    p.Size=Vector3.new(width or .2,width or .2,delta.Magnitude)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((from+to)/2,to)
    p.Parent=fxFolder
    Debris:AddItem(p,.14)
end
local function fxPulse(pos,color,radius)
    local part=Instance.new("Part")
    part.Name="PowerPulse"; part.Anchored=true; part.CanCollide=false; part.CanTouch=false; part.CanQuery=false
    part.CastShadow=false; part.Material=Enum.Material.Neon; part.Shape=Enum.PartType.Cylinder
    part.Color=color; part.Size=Vector3.new(radius*2,.12,radius*2); part.Transparency=.77
    part.CFrame=CFrame.new(pos.X,.63,pos.Z)
    part.Parent=fxFolder
    Debris:AddItem(part,.28)
end
-- Lightweight procedural attack and dodge poses; visible to other players.
-- Works on both R6 and R15 without uploaded animation asset IDs.
local baseC0 = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local poseToken = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local activePoseTween = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local function poseMotor(motor, offset, duration, recover, followthrough)
    if not motor or not motor:IsA("Motor6D") then return end
    if not baseC0[motor] then baseC0[motor] = motor.C0 end
    local serial = (poseToken[motor] or 0) + 1
    poseToken[motor] = serial
    if activePoseTween[motor] then activePoseTween[motor]:Cancel() end
    local tween = TweenService:Create(motor, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        C0 = baseC0[motor] * offset
    })
    activePoseTween[motor] = tween
    tween:Play()
    task.delay(duration + .015, function()
        if motor.Parent and poseToken[motor] == serial then
            if activePoseTween[motor] then activePoseTween[motor]:Cancel() end
            if followthrough then
                local snap=TweenService:Create(motor,TweenInfo.new(.13,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
                    C0=baseC0[motor]*followthrough})
                activePoseTween[motor]=snap
                snap:Play()
            end
            task.delay(followthrough and .15 or 0,function()
                if not motor.Parent or poseToken[motor]~=serial then return end
                if activePoseTween[motor] then activePoseTween[motor]:Cancel() end
                local back=TweenService:Create(motor,TweenInfo.new(recover,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
                    C0=baseC0[motor]})
                activePoseTween[motor]=back
                back:Play()
            end)
        end
    end)
end
-- Physically readable three-phase combat choreography for R6 and R15.
-- R15 joints are animated through their Motor6D C0 so the motions work without
-- uploaded Animation IDs; the existing rig/Animate script remains responsible
-- for idle locomotion. Each cast pose has wind-up, follow-through and recovery.
local function animateBody(character, mode, step, whipId)
    local upper=character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not upper then return end
    local right=upper:FindFirstChild("RightShoulder") or upper:FindFirstChild("Right Shoulder")
    local left=upper:FindFirstChild("LeftShoulder") or upper:FindFirstChild("Left Shoulder")
    local waist=upper:FindFirstChild("Waist")
    local lower=character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
    local rootJoint=(lower and lower:FindFirstChild("Root"))
        or (character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("RootJoint"))
    local torso=waist or rootJoint
    local follow=0.21
    if mode=="Dodge" then
        poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(42),0,math.rad(24)),.075,.24,
            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(16),0,math.rad(-20)))
        poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(42),0,math.rad(-24)),.075,.24)
        poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-20),0,math.rad(7)),.08,.24)
    elseif mode=="Heavy" then
        poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-125),math.rad(-54),math.rad(86)),.18,.29,
            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(45),math.rad(104),math.rad(-79)))
        poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(40),math.rad(12),math.rad(-56)),.16,.31,
            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-20),math.rad(-16),math.rad(18)))
        poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(16),math.rad(29),math.rad(-13)),.18,.32,
            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-19),math.rad(-27),math.rad(12)))
    elseif mode=="Power" then
        -- No ability reuses the ordinary three-hit attack pose.
        if whipId=="Rope" then -- shoulder windup, lunging front thrust
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-85),math.rad(-58),math.rad(65)),.14,.22,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(12),math.rad(112),math.rad(-67)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(36),0,math.rad(-25)),.12,.26)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(12),math.rad(22),0),.15,.24,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-23),math.rad(-18),0))
        elseif whipId=="Ember" then -- wide circular fiery sweep
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-88),math.rad(-73),math.rad(70)),.19,.31,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(29),math.rad(124),math.rad(-70)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-32),math.rad(38),math.rad(-65)),.17,.30,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(15),math.rad(-35),math.rad(52)))
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-6),math.rad(-36),0),.20,.30,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(5),math.rad(48),0))
        elseif whipId=="Frost" then -- deliberate straight icy lance
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-100),math.rad(-15),math.rad(16)),.21,.25,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(5),math.rad(90),math.rad(-30)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(25),math.rad(24),math.rad(-30)),.16,.27)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(2),math.rad(14),math.rad(-6)),.18,.27,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-12),math.rad(-11),0))
        elseif whipId=="Judgement" then -- gather both arms, raise whip, cast thunder verdict
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-165),math.rad(-22),math.rad(28)),.23,.40,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(42),math.rad(92),math.rad(-40)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-88),math.rad(23),math.rad(-29)),.20,.33)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(17),math.rad(12),0),.21,.34,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-20),math.rad(-18),0))
        elseif whipId=="Volt" then -- high raised arm, downward thunder snap
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-154),0,math.rad(24)),.19,.29,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(40),math.rad(18),math.rad(-35)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-34),0,math.rad(-26)),.17,.25)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(11),0,math.rad(-9)),.18,.30,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-18),0,math.rad(9)))
        elseif whipId=="Gravity" then -- hands raise and reel inward
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-118),math.rad(-35),math.rad(70)),.21,.32,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-34),math.rad(36),math.rad(28)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-100),math.rad(34),math.rad(-73)),.21,.32,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-36),math.rad(-31),math.rad(-25)))
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(8),0,0),.19,.29,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(18),0,0))
        elseif whipId=="Phantom" then -- compact crouch and crossing ambush
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-70),math.rad(70),math.rad(-55)),.12,.26,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(28),math.rad(-103),math.rad(60)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(28),math.rad(-35),math.rad(-8)),.13,.23)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-24),math.rad(26),math.rad(14)),.12,.29,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(17),math.rad(-28),math.rad(-13)))
        elseif whipId=="Legend" then -- overhead royal grapple
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-145),math.rad(-35),math.rad(65)),.21,.3,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(20),math.rad(120),math.rad(-65)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-45),math.rad(30),math.rad(-52)),.18,.28)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(10),math.rad(28),math.rad(-11)),.20,.28,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-14),math.rad(-21),math.rad(10)))
        else
            poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-118),0,math.rad(58)),.17,.27,
                CFrame.Angles(math.rad(20),0,math.rad(-38)))
            poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-35),0,math.rad(-33)),.14,.23)
            poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(8),0,0),.17,.26)
        end
    else
        -- Light attacks alternate left/right, with a visibly larger third finisher.
        local side=(step==2) and -1 or 1
        local power=(step==3) and 1.23 or 1
        poseMotor(right,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-79*power),math.rad(-44*side),math.rad(62*side)),.11,.26,
            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(39),math.rad(94*side),math.rad(-75*side)))
        poseMotor(left,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(14),math.rad(8*side),math.rad(-17*side)),.1,.2)
        poseMotor(torso,CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-7),math.rad(16*side),math.rad(3*side)),.11,.26,
            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(5),math.rad(-19*side),math.rad(-3*side)))
    end
end
-- Replicate only compact action timestamps, never per-frame body CFrames.
-- All clients evaluate matching attack/dodge poses for newer avatar joints.
local function markWhipMotion(character, mode, step, duration)
    character:SetAttribute("WhipMotion", mode)
    character:SetAttribute("WhipMotionStep", step)
    character:SetAttribute("WhipMotionDuration", duration)
    character:SetAttribute("WhipMotionAt", Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
    character:SetAttribute("WhipCombatUntil", Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + duration)
end

local function swingModel(character,step,mode,whipId)
    mode=mode or "Attack"
    animateBody(character,mode,step,whipId)
    local tool=character:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest") or character:FindFirstChild("LashboundWhip")
    if not tool then return end
    local handle=tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    local joint=handle and handle:FindFirstChild("WhipJoint")
    if not joint then return end
    local n=(joint:GetAttribute("SwingNo") or 0)+1
    joint:SetAttribute("SwingNo",n)
    local idle=CFrame.new(0,.95,0)
    local side=(step==2) and -1 or 1
    -- Raise the arm, visibly COIL the lash, then snap toward the target.
    -- The lash whip-tip impact timestamps (.29/.33/.37s) stay server-authoritative.
    local windup=mode=="Heavy" and .26 or mode=="Power" and .40 or .21
    local impactAngle=mode=="Heavy" and 148 or mode=="Power" and 132 or step==3 and 124 or 104
    local returnTime=mode=="Heavy" and .38 or mode=="Power" and .42 or .27
    local startYaw=(mode=="Power" and whipId=="Volt") and 20 or 66
    joint.C0=idle*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-70),math.rad(startYaw*side),math.rad(-35*side))
    local coil=TweenService:Create(joint,TweenInfo.new(windup*.63,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        C0=idle*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-100),math.rad(77*side),math.rad(-55*side))
    })
    coil:Play()
    task.delay(windup*.61,function()
        if not joint.Parent or joint:GetAttribute("SwingNo")~=n then return end
        TweenService:Create(joint,TweenInfo.new(windup*.53,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,Enum.EasingDirection.In),{
            C0=idle*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(43),math.rad(-impactAngle*side),math.rad(49*side))
        }):Play()
    end)
    task.delay(windup+.09,function()
        if joint.Parent and joint:GetAttribute("SwingNo")==n then
            TweenService:Create(joint,TweenInfo.new(returnTime,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
                C0=idle
            }):Play()
        end
    end)
end

-- Clients render brief cosmetic arcs locally; no server-spawned particle floods.
local function broadcastAction(origin, kind, direction, color, step, whipId, actorUserId, hitbox)
    local payload={kind=kind,origin=origin,direction=direction,color=color,step=step,whipId=whipId,actorUserId=actorUserId,startedAt=Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()}
    if kind=="Attack" then payload.impactServerTime=payload.startedAt+.29
    elseif kind=="Heavy" then payload.impactServerTime=payload.startedAt+.33
    elseif kind=="Power" then payload.impactServerTime=payload.startedAt+.52 end
    if hitbox then
        -- Only the server writes combat-range metadata; the client merely draws it.
        payload.hitbox=hitbox
    end
    for _,observer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local c=observer.Character
        local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if r and (r.Position-origin).Magnitude &lt; 165 then
            Feedback:FireClient(observer,"Visual",kind,payload)
        end
    end
end
local function vectorFacing(param,root)
    local face=Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X,0,root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
    if face.Magnitude&lt;.1 then face=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
    if typeof(param)~="Vector3" or param.X~=param.X or param.Z~=param.Z then return face.Unit end
    local flat=Vector3.new(param.X,0,param.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude&lt;.1 or flat.Magnitude&gt;100 then return face.Unit end
    local vec=flat.Unit
    -- Validated unit aim can point anywhere around the player. The old backwards
    -- veto silently ignored legitimate clicks/taps while the camera turned.
    return vec
end
local function allTargets(attacker)
    local out={}
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p~=attacker and isArena(p) then
            local _,h,r=charInfo(p)
            if h and r then table.insert(out,{kind="Player",player=p,root=r,hum=h,pos=r.Position}) end
        end
    end
    local active=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LashboundBattleArena")
    local practice=active and active:FindFirstChild("PracticeTargets")
    for _,target in ipairs(practice and practice:GetChildren() or {}) do
        local core=target:FindFirstChild("Core")
        local hp=dummyHealth[target]
        if core and hp and hp&gt;0 then
            table.insert(out,{kind="Dummy",model=target,root=core,pos=core.Position})
        end
    end
    -- Active sentinels are humanoid NPCs. They are eligible for the same whip
    -- hit checks as players and dummies, but rewards happen on coin pickup.
    local enemies=active and active:FindFirstChild("LashboundBots")
    for _,model in ipairs(enemies and enemies:GetChildren() or {}) do
        if model:IsA("Model") and model:GetAttribute("LashboundNPC")==true then
            local h=model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local r=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if h and h.Health&gt;0 and r then
                table.insert(out,{kind="Bot",model=model,root=r,hum=h,pos=r.Position})
            end
        end
    end
    local bosses=active and active:FindFirstChild("LashboundBosses")
    for _,model in ipairs(bosses and bosses:GetChildren() or {}) do
        local h=model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local r=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if h and h.Health&gt;0 and r then
            table.insert(out,{kind="Boss",model=model,root=r,hum=h,pos=r.Position})
        end
    end
    return out
end
local function canDamage(victim)
    return os.clock()&gt;=(victim:GetAttribute("SafeUntil") or 0) and os.clock()&gt;=(victim:GetAttribute("DodgeUntil") or 0)
end
local function stunTarget(t,duration)
    duration=math.clamp(duration,.1,2)
    local untilTime=os.clock()+duration
    if (t.kind=="Bot" or t.kind=="Boss") and t.model then
        t.model:SetAttribute("BotFrozenUntil",math.max(untilTime,t.model:GetAttribute("BotFrozenUntil") or 0))
    elseif t.kind=="Player" and t.hum and t.player then
        local player=t.player
        local maxUntil=math.max(untilTime,player:GetAttribute("StunnedUntil") or 0)
        player:SetAttribute("StunnedUntil",maxUntil)
        t.hum.WalkSpeed=0
        t.hum.JumpPower=0
        task.delay(duration+.03,function()
            if t.hum.Parent and os.clock()&gt;=(player:GetAttribute("StunnedUntil") or 0) then
                t.hum.WalkSpeed=18
                t.hum.JumpPower=52
            end
        end)
    end
end
local function tugTarget(t,origin,pullSpeed)
    if t.kind~="Bot" and t.kind~="Player" then return end
    if not t.root or not t.root.Parent then return end
    local diff=origin-t.root.Position
    local flat=Vector3.new(diff.X,0,diff.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude&gt;3 then
        t.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=flat.Unit*math.clamp(pullSpeed,20,88)+Vector3.new(0,7,0)
    end
end
local function damage(attacker,t,amount,knock,origin,slow)
    if t.kind=="Player" then
        if not canDamage(t.player) or not t.hum or t.hum.Health&lt;=0 then return false end
        local before=t.hum.Health
        lastHit[t.player]={player=attacker,at=os.clock()}
        t.player:SetAttribute("LastCombatAt",os.clock())
        attacker:SetAttribute("LastCombatAt",os.clock())
        t.hum:TakeDamage(amount)
        if knock and knock&gt;0 then
            local v=t.root.Position-origin
            v=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
            if v.Magnitude&gt;.1 then t.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=v.Unit*knock+Vector3.new(0,8,0) end
        end
        if slow then
            local hum=t.hum
            hum.WalkSpeed=9
            local finish=os.clock()+slow
            t.player:SetAttribute("SlowedUntil",finish)
            task.delay(slow,function()
                if hum.Parent and t.player:GetAttribute("SlowedUntil")==finish then hum.WalkSpeed=18 end
            end)
        end
        if before&gt;0 and t.hum.Health&lt;=0 then
            local tag=lastHit[t.player]
            if tag and tag.player==attacker and tag.at&gt;os.clock()-2 then setKO(attacker) end
            lastHit[t.player]=nil
        end
    elseif t.kind=="Bot" or t.kind=="Boss" then
        if not t.hum or t.hum.Health&lt;=0 or (t.kind=="Bot" and os.clock()&lt;(t.model:GetAttribute("BotDodgeUntil") or 0)) then return false end
        if t.kind=="Boss" and t.model:GetAttribute("BossInvulnerable")==true then return false end
        attacker:SetAttribute("LastCombatAt",os.clock())
        t.hum:SetAttribute("PlayerHitAt",os.clock())
        t.hum:SetAttribute("LastAttackerId",attacker.UserId)
        if t.kind=="Boss" and t.model:GetAttribute("BossPhase")==1 and amount&gt;=t.hum.Health then
            -- Phase one cannot enter Humanoid.Died: its first fatal strike triggers
            -- a single cinematic revival; the second phase dies normally.
            t.model:SetAttribute("BossInvulnerable",true)
            amount=math.max(0,t.hum.Health-1) -- report actual damage dealt, not massive overkill
            t.hum.Health=1
            t.model:SetAttribute("ReviveTrigger",Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
            notify(attacker,"Message","COLOSSUS FALLS... SECOND WIND AWAKENING!")
        else
            t.hum:TakeDamage(amount)
        end
        if t.kind=="Bot" and knock and knock&gt;0 then
            local delta=t.root.Position-origin
            local flat=Vector3.new(delta.X,0,delta.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude&gt;.1 then
                t.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=flat.Unit*math.min(38,knock)+Vector3.new(0,6,0)
            end
        end
    else
        local hp=dummyHealth[t.model]
        if not hp or hp&lt;=0 then return false end
        hp=math.max(0,hp-amount)
        dummyHealth[t.model]=hp
        t.model:SetAttribute("HP",hp)
        if hp==0 then
            addCoins(attacker,Config.BotReward)
            notify(attacker,"Message","DUMMY DOWN! +"..Config.BotReward.." coins")
            local core=t.model:FindFirstChild("Core")
            if core then core.Transparency=.75 end
            task.delay(3.5,function()
                if t.model.Parent then
                    dummyHealth[t.model]=100
                    t.model:SetAttribute("HP",100)
                    if core then core.Transparency=0 end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    notify(attacker,"Hit","-"..math.floor(amount),{amount=math.floor(amount),position=t.pos,kind=t.kind,heavy=(attacker.Character and attacker.Character:GetAttribute("WhipMotion")=="Heavy") or false})
    if t.pos then
        local impactKey=t.player or t.model
        local now=os.clock()
        if impactKey and now-(impactAt[impactKey] or -10)&gt;.10 then
            impactAt[impactKey]=now
            local impactDir=t.pos-origin
            if impactDir.Magnitude&lt;.2 then impactDir=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
            local whip=Whips[attacker:GetAttribute("SelectedWhip") or "Rope"] or Whips.Rope
            broadcastAction(t.pos,"Impact",impactDir.Unit,whip.Color,1,nil,attacker.UserId)
        end
    end
    return true
end
-- Each freshly cloned arena receives its own HP labels; retired dummies are discarded.
local function setupDummies(active)
    local practice=active and active:FindFirstChild("PracticeTargets")
    if not practice then return end
