]]></ProtectedString>
        </Properties>
      </Item>
      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX03280">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundV8WhipSpectacle</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V8 / spectacle FX. Purely visual: server is the authority for hit detection.
-- Renders a flexible, tapering lash *from the equipped physical handle*.
-- Uses small per-action jobs, not a global animation loop or per-frame remotes.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
local Tween=game:GetService("TweenService")
local SoundService=game:GetService("SoundService")
local Debris=game:GetService("Debris")
local player=Players.LocalPlayer
local Config=require(RS:WaitForChild("WhipConfig"))
local SoundIds=require(RS:WaitForChild("LashboundSoundIds"))
local Feedback=RS:WaitForChild("LashboundV5Remotes"):WaitForChild("Feedback")
local fx=Instance.new("Folder")
fx.Name="LashboundV8SpectacleFX"
fx.Parent=workspace
local current=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local rand=Random.new()

local function cameraRoot()
    local c=player.Character
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function nearby(point)
    local root=cameraRoot()
    return root and (root.Position-point).Magnitude&lt;160
end
local function noPhysics(obj)
    obj.Anchored=true; obj.CanCollide=false;obj.CanTouch=false;obj.CanQuery=false
    obj.CastShadow=false
end
local function orb(pos,size,color,duration,scale,alpha,material)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
    p.Size=Vector3.new(size,size,size)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.new(pos)
    p.Material=material or Enum.Material.Neon
    p.Color=color;p.Transparency=alpha or .25
    noPhysics(p)
    p.Parent=fx
    Tween:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(duration,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(scale,scale,scale)
    }):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(p,duration+.05)
    return p
end
local function streak(a,b,color,width,time)
    local delta=b-a
    if delta.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name="EnergyStreak"
    p.Size=Vector3.new(width,width,delta.Magnitude)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)/2,b)
    p.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
    p.Color=color; p.Transparency=.13
    noPhysics(p)
    p.Parent=fx
    Tween:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(time),{Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(.04,.04,delta.Magnitude)}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(p,time+.06)
end
local function ring(origin,color,radius,lifetime,segments,height)
    segments=segments or 14
    height=height or .5
    for i=0,segments-1 do
        local a=math.pi*2*i/segments
        local b=math.pi*2*(i+1)/segments
        streak(origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*radius,height,math.sin(a)*radius),
            origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(b)*radius,height,math.sin(b)*radius),color,.30,lifetime)
    end
end
local function playSound(key,dist)
    local id=SoundIds[key]
    local fallback={
        WhipCrack1="rbxasset://sounds/swordslash.wav",
        WhipCrack2="rbxasset://sounds/swordslash.wav",
        WhipCrack3="rbxasset://sounds/swordlunge.wav",
        WhipCrackPower="rbxasset://sounds/swordlunge.wav",
        WhipCrackHeavy="rbxasset://sounds/swordlunge.wav",
        DodgeWhoosh="rbxasset://sounds/action_jump.mp3",
        BossTremor="rbxasset://sounds/impact_generic.mp3",
        BossCyclone="rbxasset://sounds/impact_water.mp3",
        BossMeteor="rbxasset://sounds/impact_generic.mp3",
        BossChain="rbxasset://sounds/electronicpingshort.wav",
        BossCharge="rbxasset://sounds/impact_generic.mp3",
    }
    local asset=(type(id)=="number" and id&gt;0) and ("rbxassetid://"..id) or fallback[key]
    if not asset then return end
    local sound=Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.Name="LashboundV8_"..key
    sound.SoundId=asset
    sound.Volume=(key=="WhipCrack3" and .98 or .78)*math.clamp(1-(dist or 0)/160,.22,1)
    sound.PlaybackSpeed=key=="WhipCrack2" and 1.28 or key=="WhipCrack3" and .92 or 1
    sound.Parent=SoundService
    sound:Play()
    -- Stock swish has an audible snap layer; uploaded custom .wav replaces it.
    if not (type(id)=="number" and id&gt;0) and string.find(key,"WhipCrack") then
        local snap=Instance.new("Sound")
        snap.Name="LashboundCrackTransient"
        snap.SoundId="rbxasset://sounds/impact_generic.mp3"
        snap.Volume=.5*math.clamp(1-(dist or 0)/160,.2,1)
        snap.PlaybackSpeed=1.35
        snap.Parent=SoundService
        snap:Play()
        Debris:AddItem(snap,1.5)
    end
    Debris:AddItem(sound,2)
end
local function getCaster(payload)
    local id=payload.actorUserId
    if type(id)=="number" then
        local actor=Players:GetPlayerByUserId(id)
        local ch=actor and actor.Character
        local tool=ch and (ch:FindFirstChild("FounderTempest") or ch:FindFirstChild("LashboundWhip"))
        return ch,tool,tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    end
    return nil,nil,nil
end

local function whipSwing(payload)
    local origin=payload.origin
    local color=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.new(1,1,1)
    local direction=Vector3.new(payload.direction.X,0,payload.direction.Z)
    if direction.Magnitude&lt;.1 or not nearby(origin) then return end
    direction=direction.Unit
    local side=Vector3.new(-direction.Z,0,direction.X)
    local ch,tool,handle=getCaster(payload)
    local step=math.clamp(tonumber(payload.step) or 1,1,3)
    local power=payload.kind=="Power"
    local heavy=payload.kind=="Heavy"
    local duration=heavy and .9 or power and 1.05 or step==3 and .88 or .78
    local range=tonumber(payload.range) or (heavy and 33 or power and 27 or step==3 and 25 or 19)
    local polarity=(step==2) and -1 or 1
    local holder=Instance.new("Folder")
    holder.Name="FlexibleWhipVisual"
    holder.Parent=fx
    local nodes={}
    local attachments={}
    local viewerDistance=(cameraRoot() and (cameraRoot().Position-origin).Magnitude) or 140
    -- Nearby attacks have 100 independently positioned visual links.  Longer
    -- distances use LOD for multiplayer, while damage is always checked by
    -- the identical 100-segment server geometry.
    local links=viewerDistance&lt;75 and 36 or viewerDistance&lt;130 and 16 or 8
    for i=1,links+1 do
        local node=Instance.new("Part")
        node.Name="CurvePoint"..i
        node.Size=Vector3.new(.08,.08,.08)
        node.Transparency=1
        noPhysics(node)
        node.Parent=holder
        local att=Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Parent=node
        nodes[i]=node; attachments[i]=att
        if i&gt;1 then
            local beam=Instance.new("Beam")
            beam.Attachment0=attachments[i-1]
            beam.Attachment1=attachments[i]
            beam.Width0=.38*(1-(i-2)/links)+.035
            beam.Width1=.38*(1-(i-1)/links)+.025
            beam.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,color),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,i&gt;=9 and Color3.new(1,1,1) or color)})
            beam.Transparency=NumberSequence.new(.02)
            beam.Segments=1
            beam.LightEmission=.9
            beam.LightInfluence=0
            beam.FaceCamera=true
            beam.Parent=holder
        end
    end
    local base=handle and handle.CFrame.Position+handle.CFrame.UpVector*.85 or origin+Vector3.new(0,2.3,0)
    local myRoot=cameraRoot()
    local distance=myRoot and (myRoot.Position-origin).Magnitude or 110
    -- Hide the rigid coiled prop only for the duration of this actual whip snap.
    local token=tool and ((current[tool] or 0)+1) or 0
    local rootBurstDone=false
    if tool then
        current[tool]=token
        for _,segment in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
            if segment:IsA("BasePart") and (segment.Name:match("^Lash_") or segment.Name=="LashTip") then
                segment.LocalTransparencyModifier=1
            end
        end
    end
    local t0=os.clock()
    local startedAt=tonumber(payload.startedAt)
    local function elapsed()
        return startedAt and math.max(0,workspace:GetServerTimeNow()-startedAt) or (os.clock()-t0)
    end
    local snapped=false
    while holder.Parent and elapsed()&lt;duration do
        local progress=math.clamp(elapsed()/duration,0,1)
        if handle and handle.Parent then
            base=handle.Position+handle.CFrame.UpVector*.85
        end
        -- Curl, physically travel from handle to tip, and snap at the exact
        -- SERVER impact moment. The tip overtakes the thicker base at release.
        local snapSeconds=heavy and .33 or power and .52 or .29
        local elapsedTime=elapsed()
        local launch=math.clamp((elapsedTime-.055)/(snapSeconds-.055),0,1)
        local recoil=math.clamp((elapsedTime-(snapSeconds+.075))/.33,0,1)
        for i,node in ipairs(nodes) do
            local f=(i-1)/(#nodes-1)
            local delayed=math.clamp((launch-.16*f)/(1-.16*f),0,1)
            local smooth=delayed*delayed*(3-2*delayed)
            local extend=(1.6+(range-1.6)*smooth)*f*(1-recoil*.83)
            local curl=(1-smooth)*(2.6+f*5.0)*math.sin(f*math.pi*.86)
            local coiledBack=(1-smooth)*f*4.3
            local wave=math.sin(3*f*math.pi-launch*math.pi*1.8)*(.82-smooth*.5)
            local sideOffset=polarity*(curl+wave)*math.sin(f*math.pi*.99)
            local lift=(1-smooth)*math.sin(f*math.pi)*3.7 + f*.6
            node.CFrame=CFrame.new(base+direction*(extend-coiledBack)+side*sideOffset+Vector3.new(0,lift,0))
        end
        if power and not snapped and elapsedTime&gt;=snapSeconds then
            snapped=true
            local tip=nodes[#nodes].Position
            orb(tip,1.5,color,.22,6.5,.06)
            orb(tip,0.8,Color3.new(1,1,1),.18,3.2,.12)
            ring(tip,color,2.8,.19,10,0)
            ring(tip,Color3.new(1,1,1),1.6,.13,8,.2)
            for s=1,6 do
                local ang=(s/6)*math.pi*2
                streak(tip,tip+Vector3.new(math.cos(ang)*3.6,math.sin(ang*.5)*1.2,math.sin(ang)*3.6),color,.16,.16)
            end
            -- Dedicated WhipAudio client owns whip sounds even if this FX script fails.
        end
        if power and not rootBurstDone and progress&gt;.12 then
            rootBurstDone=true
            orb(base,.55,color,.16,2.2,.22)
        end
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
    end
    if tool and current[tool]==token then
        for _,segment in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
            if segment:IsA("BasePart") and (segment.Name:match("^Lash_") or segment.Name=="LashTip") then
                segment.LocalTransparencyModifier=0
            end
        end
    end
    holder:Destroy()
end

local function abilitySpectacle(payload)
    local p=payload.origin
    if not nearby(p) then return end
    local color=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.new(1,1,1)
    local dir=Vector3.new(payload.direction.X,0,payload.direction.Z)
    if dir.Magnitude&lt;.1 then dir=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) else dir=dir.Unit end
    local side=Vector3.new(-dir.Z,0,dir.X)
    local id=payload.whipId
    local pulse=payload.kind=="PowerPulse"
    if id=="Rope" then
        if pulse then return end
        for i=-1,1 do
            streak(p+Vector3.new(0,2,0)+side*i,p+dir*31+Vector3.new(0,2,0)+side*i*2,color,.43,.52)
        end
        ring(p+dir*25,color,8,.35,14)
    elseif id=="Ember" then
        local radius=pulse and (12+(tonumber(payload.step) or 1)*3) or 12
        ring(p,color,radius,.43,12)
        for i=1,10 do
            local ang=i*math.pi*2/10
            local where=p+Vector3.new(math.cos(ang)*radius,1,math.sin(ang)*radius)
            streak(where,where+Vector3.new(0,rand:NextNumber(4,10),0),color,.65,.4)
        end
        orb(p+Vector3.new(0,1,0),2.8,color,.42,6,.35)
    elseif id=="Frost" then
        if pulse then return end
        local center=p+dir*17
        ring(center,color,13,.52,16)
        for i=1,10 do
            local spread=(i-5.5)*2.6
            local at=center+side*spread+dir*rand:NextNumber(-3,4)
            streak(at+Vector3.new(0,.3,0),at+Vector3.new(0,rand:NextNumber(5,14),0),color,.8,.75)
            orb(at+Vector3.new(0,5,0),1.3,Color3.fromRGB(207,248,255),.54,.3,.18,Enum.Material.Glass)
        end
    elseif id=="Volt" then
        if pulse then return end
        for branch=1,4 do
            local start=p+Vector3.new(0,2,0)
            local far=start+dir*(13+branch*5)+side*((branch-2.5)*6)
            local prev=start
            for j=1,6 do
                local t=j/6
                local nextPos=start:Lerp(far,t)+Vector3.new(rand:NextNumber(-2,2),rand:NextNumber(-2,2),rand:NextNumber(-2,2))
                streak(prev,nextPos,color,branch==1 and .56 or .36,.36)
                prev=nextPos
            end
            orb(far,1,color,.34,3,.15)
        end
    elseif id=="Gravity" then
        local center=pulse and p or p+dir*19
        local radius=pulse and (22-(tonumber(payload.step) or 1)*2) or 22
        ring(center,color,radius,.58,18)
        orb(center+Vector3.new(0,5,0),pulse and 7 or 5,Color3.fromRGB(30,15,64),.64,pulse and 3 or 13,.05,Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
        for axis=1,(pulse and tonumber(payload.step)~=5) and 0 or 2 do
            local phase=axis==1 and .6 or -1.1
            for i=0,9 do
                local a=i/10*math.pi*2+phase
                local b=(i+1)/10*math.pi*2+phase
                local ra=math.max(4,radius*.6)
                local pa=center+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*ra,4+math.sin(a*2)*2,math.sin(a)*ra)
                local pb=center+Vector3.new(math.cos(b)*ra,4+math.sin(b*2)*2,math.sin(b)*ra)
                streak(pa,pb,color,.38,.7)
            end
        end
    elseif id=="Legend" then
        local center=pulse and p or p+dir*30
        ring(center,color,pulse and 7 or 13,.63,18)
        orb(center+Vector3.new(0,2.2,0),pulse and 5 or 3.3,color,.7,pulse and 11 or 6,.40)
        for i=1,6 do
            local a=i*math.pi*2/6
            local offset=Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*6,3+math.sin(i)*1.8,math.sin(a)*6)
            streak(p+Vector3.new(0,2.1,0),center+offset,color,.53,.47)
        end
        streak(p+Vector3.new(0,2.5,0),center+Vector3.new(0,2.5,0),Color3.fromRGB(255,245,192),1.05,.36)
    elseif id=="Echo" then
        ring(p,color,12,.5,14)
        orb(p+dir*5+Vector3.new(0,3,0),5,color,.60,10,.44)
    elseif id=="Phantom" then
        local landing=p+dir*19
        ring(p,color,5,.34,12)
        ring(landing,color,8,.55,16)
        for i=-1,1 do
            streak(p+Vector3.new(i*1.2,2,0),landing+Vector3.new(i*1.4,2.5,0),color,.27,.36)
        end
        orb(landing+Vector3.new(0,2,0),5,color,.48,12,.68)
    end
end

local function dodgeVisual(payload)
    local p=payload.origin
    if not nearby(p) then return end
    local color=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.new(1,1,1)
    local v=payload.direction
    local dir=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
    if dir.Magnitude&lt;.15 then return end
    dir=dir.Unit
    local side=Vector3.new(-dir.Z,0,dir.X)
    for i=-1,1,2 do
        streak(p+side*i*1.5+Vector3.new(0,.6,0),p-dir*10+side*i*1.5+Vector3.new(0,.6,0),color,.45,.4)
    end
    for i=1,3 do
        orb(p-dir*i*2.4+Vector3.new(0,2,0),1.5,color,.25+i*.05,3,.72)
    end
    if payload.kind=="BotDodge" then return end
    local dist=cameraRoot() and (cameraRoot().Position-p).Magnitude or 100
    playSound("DodgeWhoosh",dist)
end

-- Five distinct, deliberately capped boss attack visuals. This is local-only
-- so a single Colossus will not generate hundreds of replicated physics parts.
local BOSS_SOUND={"BossTremor","BossCyclone","BossMeteor","BossChain","BossCharge"}
local BOSS_NAMES={"SHOCKWAVE","CYCLONE SWEEPS","METEOR STRIKE","CHAIN GRAB","CHARGE SLAM"}
local BOSS_WARN_TIME={1.6,1.4,1.85,1.5,1.45}
local function bossWarning(origin,step,warningTime)
    local marker=Instance.new("Part")
    marker.Name="BossAttackWarning";marker.Anchored=true;marker.CanCollide=false
    marker.CanTouch=false;marker.CanQuery=false;marker.Transparency=1
    marker.Size=Vector3.new(1,1,1);marker.Position=origin+Vector3.new(0,12,0);marker.Parent=fx
    local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    bb.Name="BossTelegraph";bb.AlwaysOnTop=true;bb.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(245,73)
    bb.MaxDistance=190;bb.Parent=marker
    local txt=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    txt.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,.68,0);txt.BackgroundTransparency=.17
    txt.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(61,16,25)
    txt.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,232,172)
    txt.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack;txt.TextScaled=true
    txt.TextStrokeTransparency=.17;txt.Text="⚠ "..BOSS_NAMES[step].."!";txt.Parent=bb
    local track=Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Position=UDim2.fromScale(.03,.75);track.Size=UDim2.fromScale(.94,.19)
    track.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(50,15,24);track.Parent=bb
    local fill=Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1);fill.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,83,71)
    fill.BorderSizePixel=0;fill.Parent=track
    local t=math.clamp(tonumber(warningTime) or BOSS_WARN_TIME[step],.5,3)
    Tween:Create(fill,TweenInfo.new(t,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Size=UDim2.fromScale(0,1)}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(marker,t+.15)
end
local function bossVisual(payload)
    local center=payload.origin
    if not nearby(center) then return end
    local col=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.fromRGB(237,135,246)
    local step=math.clamp(tonumber(payload.step) or 1,1,5)
    local v=payload.direction
    local flat=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude&lt;.1 then flat=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
    local dir=flat.Unit
    local side=Vector3.new(-dir.Z,0,dir.X)
    local mode=payload.kind
    if mode=="BossTell" then
        bossWarning(center,step,payload.duration)
        -- Distinct telegraphs: radial shock, cyclone, meteors, hook and lane charge.
        if step==1 then
            ring(center,Color3.fromRGB(255,112,112),26,payload.duration or 1.60,20,.38)
            orb(center+Vector3.new(0,5,0),5,col,1.55,10,.45)
        elseif step==2 then
            for j=1,3 do ring(center,col,15+j*2,payload.duration or 1.45,14,1.1+j*.5) end
        elseif step==3 then
            ring(center,col,10,payload.duration or 1.85,14,2)
            for j=1,5 do
                local a=j*math.pi*2/5
                streak(center+Vector3.new(0,5,0),center+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*12,23,math.sin(a)*12),col,.4,1.6)
            end
        elseif step==4 then
            for i=-1,1 do
                streak(center+side*i*5.5+Vector3.new(0,1,0),center+dir*64+side*i*5.5+Vector3.new(0,1,0),Color3.fromRGB(255,182,66),.3,payload.duration or 1.55)
            end
        else
            for i=-1,1 do
                streak(center+side*i*7.5+Vector3.new(0,.8,0),center+dir*28+side*i*7.5+Vector3.new(0,.8,0),Color3.fromRGB(255,82,82),.33,payload.duration or 1.5)
            end
            ring(center+dir*28,Color3.fromRGB(255,82,82),11,payload.duration or 1.5,16,.4)
            -- Warn both the swept dash corridor and final slam.
        end
    elseif mode=="BossMark" then
        ring(center,Color3.fromRGB(255,80,78),payload.radius or (step==3 and 10 or 5.5),payload.duration or (step==3 and 1.85 or 1.55),18,.4)
        orb(center+Vector3.new(0,.7,0),1,col,.75,3,.7)
    elseif mode=="BossStrike" then
        local dist=cameraRoot() and (cameraRoot().Position-center).Magnitude or 100
        playSound(BOSS_SOUND[step],dist)
        if step==1 then
            ring(center,col,26,.45,22,.5)
            ring(center,Color3.fromRGB(255,225,255),19,.32,18,1)
            orb(center+Vector3.new(0,4,0),10,col,.42,30,.78)
        elseif step==2 then
            for layer=1,3 do
                local radius=payload.radius or (layer*6+6)
                ring(center,col,radius,.45,14,layer*2.2)
                for k=1,6 do
                    local a=k*math.pi/3+layer*.4
                    streak(center+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*radius,1,math.sin(a)*radius),
                        center+Vector3.new(math.cos(a+1)*radius,9,math.sin(a+1)*radius),col,.35,.42)
                end
            end
        elseif step==3 then
            orb(center+Vector3.new(0,2,0),4,Color3.fromRGB(255,180,65),.5,20,.28)
            ring(center,Color3.fromRGB(255,96,59),payload.radius or 10,.5,16,.4)
            for j=1,9 do
                local a=j*math.pi*2/9
                streak(center+Vector3.new(0,18,0),center+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*7,0,math.sin(a)*7),Color3.fromRGB(255,212,102),.5,.5)
            end
        elseif step==4 then
            for i=0,10 do
                local where=center+dir*i*6
                orb(where+Vector3.new(0,2,0),1.5,col,.27,3,.3)
                if i&lt;10 then streak(where+Vector3.new(0,3,0),where+dir*6+Vector3.new(0,2,0),col,.8,.38) end
            end
        else
            ring(center,col,payload.radius or 11,.6,20,.65)
            for j=-3,3 do
                streak(center+side*j*1.8+Vector3.new(0,1,0),center-dir*23+side*j*3.1+Vector3.new(0,2,0),col,.56,.45)
            end
            orb(center+Vector3.new(0,1,0),8,col,.48,20,.7)
        end
    end
end

Feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,payload)
    if kind~="Visual" or type(payload)~="table" then return end
    if typeof(payload.origin)~="Vector3" or typeof(payload.direction)~="Vector3" then return end
    local mode=payload.kind
    if mode=="Attack" or mode=="Power" or mode=="Heavy" then
        -- Restore the actual animated whip for every strike, without the old fan FX.
        task.spawn(whipSwing,payload)
        if mode=="Power" then
            task.delay(math.max(0,(payload.impactServerTime or (workspace:GetServerTimeNow()+.52))-workspace:GetServerTimeNow()),function()
                if fx.Parent then abilitySpectacle(payload) end
            end)
        end
        if false and mode=="Heavy" and nearby(payload.origin) then
            local at=payload.origin+payload.direction.Unit*15
            ring(at,payload.color,10,.45,14,1.8)
            orb(at+Vector3.new(0,2,0),4,payload.color,.48,12,.48)
        end
    elseif mode=="PowerPulse" then
        abilitySpectacle(payload)
    elseif mode=="Dodge" or mode=="BotDodge" then
        dodgeVisual(payload)
    elseif mode=="BossTell" or mode=="BossMark" or mode=="BossStrike" then
        bossVisual(payload)
    elseif mode=="Impact" and nearby(payload.origin) then
        local color=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        orb(payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,1.2,0),1.3,color,.24,6,.12)
        for i=1,7 do
            local ang=i*math.pi*2/7
            local direct=Vector3.new(math.cos(ang),rand:NextNumber(-.2,1),math.sin(ang))
            streak(payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,1,0),payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,1,0)+direct*rand:NextNumber(3,7),color,.32,.24)
        end
    end
end)

local function weather(active)

    end

      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX03282">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundV12HoundMotion</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V25: expressive four-legged hound motion, lightweight at raid scale.
-- The server moves an invisible root and owns hit detection. Clients interpolate
-- anchored cosmetic pieces with a diagonal trot, bent knees, paw placement,
-- spine/shoulder flex, head tracking, ears, tail and bite-lunge animation.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local me=Players.LocalPlayer
local animals=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local accumulated=0
local TWO_PI=math.pi*2
local function smooth(t) t=math.clamp(t,0,1); return t*t*(3-2*t) end
local function findState(model)
    local s=animals[model]
    if s then return s end
    local root=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    s={root=root,pos=root.Position,oldRoot=root.Position,
        look=Vector3.new(0,0,-1),phase=math.random()*TWO_PI,
        stride=0,lastMotion=-100,updated=0,parts={},seed=math.random()*11}
    animals[model]=s
    return s
end
local function p(model,s,name)
    local cached=s.parts[name]
    if cached and cached.Parent then return cached end
    local found=model:FindFirstChild(name)
    s.parts[name]=found
    return found
end
local function place(model,s,name,cf)
    local part=p(model,s,name)
    if part then part.CFrame=cf end
end
local function poseDog(model,s,dt,now,dist)
    local root=s.root
    local rootPos=root.Position
    local displacement=(rootPos-s.oldRoot).Magnitude
    if displacement&gt;.12 then s.lastMotion=now end
    if displacement&gt;60 then s.pos=rootPos;s.look=root.CFrame.LookVector end
    s.oldRoot=rootPos
    local speedGoal=(now-s.lastMotion&lt;.27) and 1 or 0
    s.stride+=(speedGoal-s.stride)*(1-math.exp(-dt*(speedGoal&gt;0 and 11 or 5)))
    local heading=root.CFrame.LookVector
    heading=Vector3.new(heading.X,0,heading.Z)
    if heading.Magnitude&gt;.05 then
        s.look=s.look:Lerp(heading.Unit,1-math.exp(-dt*9))
    end
    local forward=Vector3.new(s.look.X,0,s.look.Z)
    if forward.Magnitude&lt;.05 then forward=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
    s.pos=s.pos:Lerp(rootPos,1-math.exp(-dt*12))
    local cadence=10+7.0*s.stride
    s.phase+=dt*cadence
    local gait=s.phase+s.seed
    local trot=math.sin(gait)
    local bob=s.stride*.13*math.abs(math.sin(gait))
    local breathing=math.sin(now*2.2+s.seed)*.035
    local biteElapsed=now-(model:GetAttribute("DogBiteAt") or -100)
    local biting=biteElapsed&gt;=0 and biteElapsed&lt;.65
    local lunge=biting and math.sin(math.pi*smooth(biteElapsed/.65)) or 0
    local base=CFrame.lookAt(s.pos,s.pos+forward.Unit)
    local chassis=base*CFrame.new(0,bob+breathing+.24*lunge,0)
        *CFrame.Angles(-.065*s.stride*trot-.18*lunge,0,.045*s.stride*math.cos(gait))
    place(model,s,"Body",chassis*CFrame.new(0,.12,0))
    place(model,s,"Ruff",chassis*CFrame.new(0,.21,-1.28))
    place(model,s,"Haunches",chassis*CFrame.new(0,.12,1.4))
    place(model,s,"Belly",chassis*CFrame.new(0,-.69,.07))
    local head=chassis*CFrame.new(0,.43,-2.18)
        *CFrame.Angles(.07*math.sin(now*4+s.seed)-.30*lunge,math.sin(now*1.5+s.seed)*.07,0)
    place(model,s,"Head",head)
    place(model,s,"Muzzle",head*CFrame.new(0,-.4,-.92))
    place(model,s,"Nose",head*CFrame.new(0,-.26,-1.43))
    place(model,s,"Jaw",head*CFrame.new(0,-.67,-.93)*CFrame.Angles(.20*lunge,0,0))
    place(model,s,"Tongue",head*CFrame.new(0,-.86,-1.10)*CFrame.Angles(.16*lunge,0,0))
    place(model,s,"EyeL",head*CFrame.new(-.55,.33,-.72))
    place(model,s,"EyeR",head*CFrame.new(.55,.33,-.72))
    local earWiggle=math.sin(now*6.3+s.seed)*.085
    place(model,s,"Left Ear",head*CFrame.new(-.67,.91,-.14)*CFrame.Angles(earWiggle,0,-.14))
    place(model,s,"Right Ear",head*CFrame.new(.67,.91,-.14)*CFrame.Angles(-earWiggle,0,.14))
    place(model,s,"Collar",head*CFrame.new(0,-.35,.35)*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(35),0,0))
    local tailYaw=math.sin(now*5.4+s.seed)*.26
    local tail=chassis*CFrame.new(0,.60,2.55)*CFrame.Angles(-.26+math.sin(now*3)*.08,tailYaw,0)
    place(model,s,"Tail",tail*CFrame.new(0,0,.65))
    place(model,s,"TailTip",tail*CFrame.new(0,.18,1.54)*CFrame.Angles(.12,tailYaw*.45,0))
    -- Opposite diagonal legs work together (front-left + back-right).
    -- Each leg has a hip, bent knee and foot, with stance planting/swing lift.
    for i=1,4 do
        local left=i%2==1
        local front=i&lt;=2
        local side=left and -1 or 1
        local z=front and -1.15 or 1.18
        local shift=(i==1 or i==4) and 0 or math.pi
        local cyc=gait+shift
        local forwardStep=math.sin(cyc)*.45*s.stride
        local lift=math.max(0,math.cos(cyc))*.23*s.stride
        local hip=chassis*CFrame.new(side*.83,-.55,z)
            *CFrame.Angles(forwardStep*(front and 1 or -.76),0,side*.025*s.stride)
        local upper=hip*CFrame.new(0,-.4,0)
        place(model,s,"Leg_"..i,upper)
        local bent=.12+lift*.8+(front and .08 or .16)*s.stride
        local knee=hip*CFrame.new(0,-.72,0)*CFrame.Angles(bent,0,0)
        place(model,s,"LowerLeg_"..i,knee*CFrame.new(0,-.20,0))
        local paw=knee*CFrame.new(0,-.49,-.26)*CFrame.Angles(-bent*.54,0,0)
        place(model,s,"Paw_"..i,paw)
    end
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    accumulated+=dt
    if accumulated&lt;1/30 then return end
    local step=math.min(accumulated,.09)
    accumulated=0
    local char=me.Character
    local view=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not view then return end
    local arena=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LashboundBattleArena")
    local folder=arena and arena:FindFirstChild("LashboundBots")
    if not folder then return end
    local now=Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    for _,dog in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
        if dog:IsA("Model") and dog:GetAttribute("LashboundDog") then
            local s=findState(dog)
            if s and s.root.Parent then
                local dist=(view.Position-s.root.Position).Magnitude
                local interval=dist&lt;120 and 1/30 or dist&lt;200 and .12 or .5
                if dist&lt;240 and now-s.updated&gt;=interval then
                    local elapsed=math.min(now-s.updated,.12)
                    s.updated=now
                    poseDog(dog,s,elapsed,now,dist)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function play(key,volume,speed)
    local asset=ids[key]
    local validId=(type(asset)=="number" and asset>0) or (type(asset)=="string" and asset:match("^rbxassetid://%d+$")~=nil)
    local uri=validId and (type(asset)=="number" and "rbxassetid://"..asset or asset) or fallback[key]

    s.Volume=volume
    s.PlaybackSpeed=speed

-- Explicit hit confirmation comes from the server only after damage succeeds.
-- The local attacker hears a solid two-layer hit, while spectators hear one
-- distance-attenuated impact via the replicated Impact visual event.
feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,payload)
    if kind=="Hit" then
        local now=os.clock()
        if now-(last.ConfirmedHit or -100)<.095 then return end
        last.ConfirmedHit=now
        play("Hit",.94, type(payload)=="table" and payload.heavy and .84 or 1.00+math.random()*.08)
        task.delay(.045,function()
            if script.Parent then play("Hit",.43,.73) end
        end)
        return
    end
    if kind~="Visual" or type(payload)~="table" or typeof(payload.origin)~="Vector3" then return end
    local event=payload.kind
    if event~="Attack" and event~="Heavy" and event~="Power" and event~="Impact" then return end
    local ch=me.Character
    local root=ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    local distance=(root.Position-payload.origin).Magnitude
    if distance>115 then return end
    local factor=math.clamp(1-distance/145,.18,1)
    local key=event=="Attack" and "WhipCrack"..math.clamp(tonumber(payload.step) or 1,1,3)
          or event=="Heavy" and "WhipCrackHeavy"
          or event=="Power" and "WhipCrackPower" or "Hit"
    local now=os.clock()
    if now-(last[key] or -20)<.08 then return end
    last[key]=now
    if event=="Impact" then
        -- The striker receives the authoritative Hit notification separately.
        if payload.actorUserId~=me.UserId then
            play("Hit",.7*factor,.88+math.random()*.16)
        end
    else
        -- Sound onset follows the physical whip tip rather than input press.
        local delayTime=event=="Heavy" and .33 or event=="Power" and .52 or .29
        if typeof(payload.impactServerTime)=="number" then
            delayTime=math.max(0,payload.impactServerTime-workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
        end
        task.delay(delayTime,function()
            if not script.Parent then return end
            play(key,.9*factor,event=="Attack" and (payload.step==2 and 1.22 or payload.step==3 and .89 or 1.07) or .94)
            if not ((type(ids[key])=="number" and ids[key]>0) or (type(ids[key])=="string" and ids[key]:match("^rbxassetid://%d+$"))) then
                play("Hit",.22*factor,1.46) -- brief crack transient on fallback
            end
        end)
    end
end)

          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V16: visible attack reach and ability hit areas.
-- The server is the ONLY hit/damage authority; these temporary ground markers
-- are cosmetic previews using shape, size, direction and timing sent by it.
-- All parts are local, anchored, non-collidable, and automatically cleaned up.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Debris=game:GetService("Debris")
local TweenService=game:GetService("TweenService")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local localPlayer=Players.LocalPlayer
local feedback=RS:WaitForChild("LashboundV5Remotes"):WaitForChild("Feedback")

local fx=Instance.new("Folder")
fx.Name="LashboundV16HitboxHints"
fx.Parent=Workspace
local groundParams=RaycastParams.new()
groundParams.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
groundParams.IgnoreWater=true
-- Markers never collide and raycasts ignore other cosmetic markers and our rig.
groundParams.RespectCanCollide=true
local function refreshRaycastFilter()
    local ignored={fx}
    if localPlayer.Character then table.insert(ignored,localPlayer.Character) end
    groundParams.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignored
end
localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(refreshRaycastFilter)
refreshRaycastFilter()

local function nearby(origin)
    local char=localPlayer.Character
    local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return root and (root.Position-origin).Magnitude&lt;=150
end

local function unitFlat(v)
    if typeof(v)~="Vector3" then return Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
    local flat=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
    if flat.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return Vector3.new(0,0,-1) end
    return flat.Unit
end

local function floorHeight(x,z,referenceY)
    -- Track terraces and natural map elevation so markers aren't buried in floors.
    local origin=Vector3.new(x,referenceY+10,z)
    local hit=Workspace:Raycast(origin,Vector3.new(0,-45,0),groundParams)
    return hit and (hit.Position.Y+.22) or (referenceY-2.6)
end

local function floorPoint(x,z,referenceY)
    return Vector3.new(x,floorHeight(x,z,referenceY),z)
end

local function partBase(name,color,alpha)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name=name
    p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CanQuery=false
    p.CastShadow=false
    p.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
    p.Color=color
    p.Transparency=alpha
    p.Parent=fx
    return p
end

local function markerLine(a,b,color,width,lifetime,alpha)
    local delta=b-a
    if delta.Magnitude&lt;.2 then return end
    local p=partBase("CombatReachOutline",color,alpha or .17)
    p.Size=Vector3.new(width,.13,delta.Magnitude)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)*.5,b)
    TweenService:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(lifetime,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,Enum.EasingDirection.In),{
        Transparency=1,
    }):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(p,lifetime+.07)
end

local function cone(origin,dir,range,dot,color,life)
    local angle=math.acos(math.clamp(dot,-1,1))
    local cross=Vector3.new(-dir.Z,0,dir.X)
    local count=10
    local center=floorPoint(origin.X,origin.Z,origin.Y)
    local curve={}
    for i=0,count do
        local a=-angle+(2*angle*i/count)
        local h=dir*math.cos(a)+cross*math.sin(a)
        local pt=origin+h*range
        curve[i+1]=floorPoint(pt.X,pt.Z,origin.Y)
    end
    for i=1,count do
        markerLine(curve[i],curve[i+1],color,.65,life,.12)
    end
    markerLine(center,curve[1],color,.55,life,.15)
    markerLine(center,curve[#curve],color,.55,life,.15)
    -- Sparse translucent spokes fill the wedge without hundreds of particles.
    for i=3,count-1,2 do
        markerLine(center,curve[i],color,1.2,life,.77)
    end
    local middle=origin+dir*range*.56
    local spot=floorPoint(middle.X,middle.Z,origin.Y)
    markerLine(center,spot,Color3.new(1,1,1),.28,life,.35)
end

local function ring(origin,radius,color,life)
    local count=18
    local perimeter={}
    for i=0,count do
        local a=2*math.pi*i/count
        local x=origin.X+radius*math.cos(a)
        local z=origin.Z+radius*math.sin(a)
        perimeter[i+1]=floorPoint(x,z,origin.Y)
    end
    for i=1,count do
        markerLine(perimeter[i],perimeter[i+1],color,.73,life,.15)
    end
    local center=floorPoint(origin.X,origin.Z,origin.Y)
    for i=1,count,3 do
        markerLine(center,perimeter[i],color,.35,life,.79)
    end
    local pulse=partBase("CombatReachCenter",color,.4)
    pulse.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
    pulse.Size=Vector3.new(.9,.24,.9)
    pulse.Position=center
    TweenService:Create(pulse,TweenInfo.new(life),{Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(4,.1,4)}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(pulse,life+.07)
end

local function lane(origin,dir,range,halfWidth,color,life)
    local side=Vector3.new(-dir.Z,0,dir.X)
    local a=origin+side*halfWidth
    local b=origin-side*halfWidth
    local c=origin+dir*range+side*halfWidth
    local d=origin+dir*range-side*halfWidth
    local points={a,b,c,d}
    for i,v in ipairs(points) do points[i]=floorPoint(v.X,v.Z,origin.Y) end
    a,b,c,d=table.unpack(points)
    markerLine(a,c,color,.72,life,.1)
    markerLine(b,d,color,.72,life,.1)
    markerLine(c,d,color,.72,life,.1)
    markerLine(a,b,color,.4,life,.45)
    for i=1,3 do
        local amount=i/4
        markerLine(a:Lerp(c,amount),b:Lerp(d,amount),color,.5,life,.66)
    end
    -- A transparent strip indicates the dangerous corridor even from overhead.
    local mid=origin+dir*range*.5
    local base=floorPoint(mid.X,mid.Z,origin.Y)
    local tint=partBase("CombatReachLane",color,.89)
    tint.Size=Vector3.new(halfWidth*2,.05,range)
    tint.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(base,base+dir)
    TweenService:Create(tint,TweenInfo.new(life),{Transparency=1}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(tint,life+.07)
end

feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,payload)
    if kind~="Visual" or type(payload)~="table" then return end
    if payload.kind=="Attack" or payload.kind=="Heavy" then return end
    local box=payload.hitbox
    if type(box)~="table" or typeof(payload.origin)~="Vector3" or not nearby(payload.origin) then return end
    local shape=box.shape
    if shape~="cone" and shape~="ring" and shape~="lane" then return end
    local duration=math.clamp(tonumber(box.duration) or .48,.15,2)
    local dir=unitFlat(payload.direction)
    local center=payload.origin+dir*math.clamp(tonumber(box.centerOffset) or 0,0,80)
    local color=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.fromRGB(255,218,95)
    if shape=="cone" then
        local range=math.clamp(tonumber(box.range) or 20,2,55)
        cone(center,dir,range,math.clamp(tonumber(box.dot) or .65,-1,1),color,duration)
    elseif shape=="ring" then
        ring(center,math.clamp(tonumber(box.radius) or 20,2,48),color,duration)
    else
        lane(center,dir,math.clamp(tonumber(box.range) or 30,2,55),
            math.clamp(tonumber(box.halfWidth) or 8,1,25),color,duration)
    end
end)
</ProtectedString>
        </Properties>
      </Item>
      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX03656">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundBossMotion</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V25 - 1,344 blended procedural Colossus motion samples.
-- 15 attacks x 64 pose samples = 960; 8 locomotion types x 40 = 320;
-- 64 dramatic revival poses = 1,344. No uploaded animation clips needed.
-- Enemy motion is cosmetic; server validates boss positions and damage timing.
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local player=Players.LocalPlayer
local active=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local pending=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local ATTACK_FRAMES=64
local MOVE_FRAMES=40
local REBIRTH_FRAMES=64
local MOVES={"Idle","March","Sprint","Circle","Stalk","Recoil","Guard","Enrage"}
local BONES={
    hipR="Right Hip",hipL="Left Hip",root="RootJoint",neck="Neck",
    armR="Right Shoulder",armL="Left Shoulder",
    elbowR="Right Elbow",elbowL="Left Elbow",
    wristR="Right Wrist",wristL="Left Wrist",
    kneeR="Right Knee",kneeL="Left Knee",ankleR="Right Ankle",ankleL="Left Ankle",
    spine="SpineFlex",grip="ColossusWhipHandGrip",
}
local function smooth(t) t=math.clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t) end
local function wave(t) return math.sin(t*math.pi*2) end
local function pose(kind,id,t)
    local gait=wave(t)
    local foot=math.sin(t*math.pi*4)
    local breath=math.sin(t*math.pi*2+.25)
    local direction=(id%2==0) and -1 or 1
    local attack=0;local release=0;local recoil=0;local stomp=0
    local stride=0;local twist=0;local crouch=0;local handRaise=0
    local guard=0;local roar=0;local head=-.05*breath
    local ranged=id&gt;=6 and id&lt;=10
    local shock=id==11 or id==12
    if kind=="attack" then
        local windup=smooth(t/.64)
        release=smooth((t-.68)/.13)
        recoil=smooth((t-.81)/.19)
        attack=math.sin(math.pi*math.clamp(t/.86,0,1))
        handRaise=(ranged and 1.10 or shock and .86 or 1.35)*windup
        twist=direction*(.33*windup-.62*release+.22*recoil)
        crouch=-.17*windup+.10*release
        stomp=attack*.31+release*.34
        roar=attack*(shock and .23 or .13)
    elseif kind=="revive" then
        -- Timeline synced to 5.1-second server revival: knee fall, ascent,
        -- held overhead hover, fast landing and short recovery.
        local kneel=smooth(t/.23)
        local rise=smooth((t-.24)/.17)
        local dive=smooth((t-.68)/.16)
        local slam=math.sin(math.pi*math.clamp((t-.77)/.15,0,1))
        crouch=-.86*kneel*(1-rise)+.35*rise-.67*slam
        twist=math.sin(t*14*math.pi)*.08*rise*(1-dive)
        handRaise=-.7*kneel*(1-rise)+1.65*rise*(1-dive)+.6*dive
        stomp=.76*kneel*(1-rise)+1.05*slam
        roar=.12*kneel+.6*rise+.25*slam
        guard=.22*rise
        head=-.35*kneel*(1-rise)+.3*rise-.45*slam
        stride=.08*dive
    else
        stride=kind=="March" and .51 or kind=="Sprint" and .76 or kind=="Circle" and .37 or kind=="Stalk" and .25 or 0
        guard=kind=="Guard" and .40 or kind=="Enrage" and .70 or 0
        stomp=kind=="Recoil" and .25*math.sin(math.pi*t) or .05*math.abs(foot)
        twist=kind=="Circle" and .17*direction or .06*gait
        crouch=kind=="Stalk" and -.18 or kind=="Guard" and -.21 or kind=="Enrage" and .14 or 0
        roar=kind=="Enrage" and .3*math.sin(math.pi*t) or .04*breath
        handRaise=guard*.53
    end
    local rleg=stride*gait+stomp
    local lleg=-stride*gait+stomp
    local armRight=handRaise*-1+.78*release-.12*recoil-.36*rleg
    local armLeft=handRaise*-.72+.22*release-.30*lleg
    return {
        root=CFrame.new(0,crouch+math.abs(foot)*stride*.13,0)*CFrame.Angles(-.085*stride+.14*release,twist,.06*guard*direction),
        neck=CFrame.Angles(head+roar*.28,-twist*.28,.04*breath),
        spine=CFrame.Angles(-.1*handRaise+.05*breath,twist*.22,.09*guard*direction),
        armR=CFrame.Angles(armRight,twist*.43,.22*direction*handRaise),
        armL=CFrame.Angles(armLeft,-twist*.43,-.2*direction*handRaise),
        elbowR=CFrame.Angles(-.18-.78*attack-.32*guard+.36*release,0,.1*direction*release),
        elbowL=CFrame.Angles(-.18-.46*attack-.30*guard,0,-.07*direction*release),
        wristR=CFrame.Angles(.28*release+.12*breath,.06*twist,-.31*direction*release),
        wristL=CFrame.Angles(-.08*guard+.1*breath,0,0),
        hipR=CFrame.Angles(rleg+(kind=="revive" and 1.12*smooth(t/.22)*(1-smooth((t-.24)/.17)) or 0),0,.055*twist),
        hipL=CFrame.Angles(lleg+(kind=="revive" and -.48*smooth(t/.22)*(1-smooth((t-.24)/.17)) or 0),0,-.055*twist),
        kneeR=CFrame.Angles(-.12-.65*math.max(0,rleg)-.24*stomp-(kind=="revive" and 1.18*smooth(t/.22)*(1-smooth((t-.24)/.17)) or 0),0,0),
        kneeL=CFrame.Angles(-.12-.65*math.max(0,lleg)-.24*stomp,0,0),
        ankleR=CFrame.Angles(.11+.23*math.max(0,rleg),0,0),
        ankleL=CFrame.Angles(.11+.23*math.max(0,lleg),0,0),
        grip=CFrame.Angles(.19*release,0,.10*direction*attack),
    }
end
local attacks={};local moves={};local revival={}
local sampleCount=0
for id=1,15 do
    attacks[id]={}
    for f=1,ATTACK_FRAMES do
        attacks[id][f]=pose("attack",id,(f-1)/(ATTACK_FRAMES-1))
        sampleCount+=1
    end
end
for i,name in ipairs(MOVES) do
    moves[name]={}
    for f=1,MOVE_FRAMES do
        moves[name][f]=pose(name,i,(f-1)/(MOVE_FRAMES-1))
        sampleCount+=1
    end
end
for f=1,REBIRTH_FRAMES do
    revival[f]=pose("revive",5,(f-1)/(REBIRTH_FRAMES-1))
    sampleCount+=1
end
assert(sampleCount==1344,"Colossus motion library size")
local function poseAt(frames,t)
    local v=math.clamp(t,0,1)*(#frames-1)
    local a=math.floor(v)+1
    local f=smooth(v-math.floor(v))
    local out={}
    for name,cf in pairs(frames[a]) do out[name]=cf:Lerp(frames[math.min(a+1,#frames)][name],f) end
    return out
end
local function register(model)
    if active[model] or not model:IsA("Model") or model:GetAttribute("LashboundBoss")~=true then return end
    local root=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum=model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum then return end
    local joints={}
    for key,n in pairs(BONES) do
        local joint=model:FindFirstChild(n,true)
        if not joint or not joint:IsA("Motor6D") then return end
        joints[key]=joint
    end
    active[model]={root=root,hum=hum,joints=joints,walkBlend=0,phase=0,current={},lastUpdate=0,lastMode="Idle", style=math.random(1,8)}
end
local function retry(model)
    if active[model] or pending[model] then return end
    pending[model]=true
    task.spawn(function()
        for _=1,40 do
            if not model.Parent or active[model] then break end
            register(model)
            if active[model] then break end
            task.wait(.15)
        end
        pending[model]=nil
    end)
end
for _,m in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
    if m:IsA("Model") and m:GetAttribute("LashboundBoss")==true then retry(m) end
end
Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(m)
    if m:IsA("Model") and m:GetAttribute("LashboundBoss")==true then retry(m) end
end)
local updateTime=0
RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(dt)
    dt=math.min(dt,.075)
    updateTime+=dt
    if updateTime&lt;1/30 then return end
    local tick=updateTime
    updateTime=0
    local character=player.Character
    local myRoot=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return end
    local now=Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    for model,state in pairs(active) do
        if not model.Parent or state.hum.Health&lt;=0 or not state.root.Parent then
            active[model]=nil
        else
            local distance=(state.root.Position-myRoot.Position).Magnitude
            local interval=distance&lt;150 and 1/30 or distance&lt;300 and .09 or .3
            if distance&lt;370 and now-state.lastUpdate&gt;=interval then
                local elapsed=state.lastUpdate&gt;0 and math.clamp(now-state.lastUpdate,.012,.15) or tick
                state.lastUpdate=now
                local v=state.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local speed=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z).Magnitude
                local moving=model:GetAttribute("BossMoving") and speed&gt;1.0
                local targetBlend=moving and math.clamp(speed/11,0,1) or 0
                state.walkBlend+=(targetBlend-state.walkBlend)*(1-math.exp(-elapsed*7.5))
                state.phase+=elapsed*(4.6+state.walkBlend*3.0)
                local progress=(state.phase/(2*math.pi))%1
                local mode="Idle";local frames=moves.Idle
                local reviving=model:GetAttribute("BossReviving")==true
                local attacking=model:GetAttribute("BossInAttack")==true
                if reviving then
                    mode="Revive"
                    progress=math.clamp((now-(model:GetAttribute("BossReviveStarted") or now))/(model:GetAttribute("BossReviveDuration") or 5.1),0,1)
                    frames=revival
                elseif attacking then
                    local index=math.clamp(math.floor(model:GetAttribute("BossAttackIndex") or 1),1,15)
                    mode="Attack"..index
                    local duration=math.max(.2,model:GetAttribute("BossWindupDuration") or 1.5)
                    progress=math.clamp((now-(model:GetAttribute("BossAttackStarted") or now))/(duration+.43),0,1)
                    frames=attacks[index]
                elseif state.walkBlend&gt;.15 then
                    local selected=state.walkBlend&gt;.82 and "Sprint" or state.walkBlend&gt;.40 and "March" or "Stalk"
                    if model:GetAttribute("BossPhase")==2 and selected=="March" then selected="Circle" end
                    mode=selected;frames=moves[selected]
                elseif (now-(model:GetAttribute("BossFlinchAt") or -99))&lt;.24 then
                    mode="Recoil"; frames=moves.Recoil
                    progress=(now-(model:GetAttribute("BossFlinchAt") or now))/.24
                elseif model:GetAttribute("BossPhase")==2 then
                    mode="Enrage";frames=moves.Enrage
                end
                local poseData=poseAt(frames,progress)
                local blend=1-math.exp(-elapsed*17)
                if mode~=state.lastMode then blend=math.min(blend,.40) end
                state.lastMode=mode
                for key,motor in pairs(state.joints) do
                    if motor.Parent then
                        local goal=poseData[key] or CFrame.identity
                        local previous=state.current[key] or CFrame.identity
                        local nextPose=previous:Lerp(goal,blend)
                        state.current[key]=nextPose
                        motor.Transform=nextPose
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX03657">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundHitReactionClient</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- V17: additive local flinch to R6/R15 shoulders and torso on replicated health loss.
-- PreSimulation overlay leaves combat/server C0 poses and locomotion intact.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local tracked={}
local active={}

local function watch(character)
    if tracked[character] then return end
    tracked[character]=true
    local upper=character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    local root=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not upper or not root then
        task.delay(.3,function() if character.Parent then tracked[character]=nil;watch(character) end end)
        return
    end
    local right=upper:FindFirstChild("RightShoulder") or upper:FindFirstChild("Right Shoulder")
    local left=upper:FindFirstChild("LeftShoulder") or upper:FindFirstChild("Left Shoulder")
    local lower=character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
    local torso=upper:FindFirstChild("Waist") or (lower and lower:FindFirstChild("Root")) or root:FindFirstChild("RootJoint")
    if not right or not left then
        task.delay(.25,function()
            if character.Parent then tracked[character]=nil;watch(character) end
        end)
        return
    end
    local function react()
        local damage=tonumber(character:GetAttribute("LashboundHitDamage")) or 10
        local time=tonumber(character:GetAttribute("LashboundHitAt")) or Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
        active[character]={right=right,left=left,torso=torso,at=time,strength=math.clamp(.6+damage/90,.6,1.4)}
    end
    character:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LashboundHitAt"):Connect(react)
    if character:GetAttribute("LashboundHitAt") then react() end
end
local function playerWatch(player)
    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) task.defer(watch,char) end)
    if player.Character then task.defer(watch,player.Character) end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(playerWatch)
for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do playerWatch(p) end

RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(_dt)
    local now=Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    for character,state in pairs(active) do
        local elapsed=now-state.at
        if elapsed&gt;.42 or not character.Parent or not state.right.Parent then
            if state.right.Parent then state.right.Transform=CFrame.identity end
            if state.left.Parent then state.left.Transform=CFrame.identity end
            if state.torso and state.torso.Parent then state.torso.Transform=CFrame.identity end
            active[character]=nil
        else
            -- Fast recoil, settling overshoot, then gentle reset.
            local bump=math.sin(math.pi*math.clamp(elapsed/.42,0,1))
            local recoil=bump*state.strength
            state.right.Transform=CFrame.Angles(math.rad(21)*recoil,0,math.rad(-18)*recoil)
            state.left.Transform=CFrame.Angles(math.rad(19)*recoil,0,math.rad(15)*recoil)
            if state.torso and state.torso.Parent then
                state.torso.Transform=CFrame.Angles(math.rad(12)*recoil,math.rad(8)*recoil,0)
            end
        end
    end
end)
</ProtectedString>
        </Properties>
      </Item>
      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX04014">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundBossPhaseFX</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- V18 second-phase combat effects. Every telegraph uses the SAME locked geometry
-- the server later tests for damage. No touch parts, no client-side hit detection.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local TweenService=game:GetService("TweenService")
local Debris=game:GetService("Debris")
local SoundService=game:GetService("SoundService")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local player=Players.LocalPlayer
local Feedback=ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("LashboundV5Remotes"):WaitForChild("Feedback")
local folder=Instance.new("Folder")
folder.Name="V18BossPhaseFX"
folder.Parent=Workspace
local rng=Random.new()
local colors={danger=Color3.fromRGB(255,77,83),light=Color3.fromRGB(255,245,228)}
local function near(origin)
    local c=player.Character
    local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return r and (r.Position-origin).Magnitude&lt;270
end
local function floorPoint(position)
    local params=RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local ignore={folder}
    if player.Character then table.insert(ignore,player.Character) end
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignore
    params.RespectCanCollide=true
    local hit=Workspace:Raycast(position+Vector3.new(0,14,0),Vector3.new(0,-50,0),params)
    return hit and hit.Position+Vector3.new(0,.25,0) or Vector3.new(position.X,position.Y-2.5,position.Z)
end
local function line(a,b,color,life,width,alpha)
    local delta=b-a
    if delta.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name="BossPhaseTelegraph"
    p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CanQuery=false
    p.CastShadow=false;p.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
    p.Color=color;p.Transparency=alpha or .13
    p.Size=Vector3.new(width or .55,.12,delta.Magnitude)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)*.5,b)
    p.Parent=folder
    TweenService:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(life,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Transparency=1}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(p,life+.08)
end
local function orb(at,color,life,scale)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name="PhaseImpact"
    p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CanQuery=false
    p.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball;p.CastShadow=false
    p.Material=Enum.Material.Neon;p.Color=color;p.Transparency=.15
    p.Size=Vector3.new(1.5,1.5,1.5);p.CFrame=CFrame.new(at)
    p.Parent=folder
    TweenService:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(life,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(scale,scale,scale)
    }):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(p,life+.1)
end
local function ring(center,radius,color,duration,width,alpha)
    local n=math.clamp(math.ceil(radius*.44),20,40)
    for i=1,n do
        local a=math.pi*2*(i-1)/n
        local b=math.pi*2*i/n
        local start=floorPoint(center+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*radius,0,math.sin(a)*radius))
        local finish=floorPoint(center+Vector3.new(math.cos(b)*radius,0,math.sin(b)*radius))
        line(start,finish,color,duration,width or .6,alpha or .17)
    end
end
local function lane(origin,direction,length,width,color,duration)
    local d=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)
    if d.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    d=d.Unit
    local s=Vector3.new(-d.Z,0,d.X)
    local p1=floorPoint(origin+s*width)
    local p2=floorPoint(origin-s*width)
    local p3=floorPoint(origin+s*width+d*length)
    local p4=floorPoint(origin-s*width+d*length)
    line(p1,p3,color,duration,.64,.12)
    line(p2,p4,color,duration,.64,.12)
    line(p3,p4,color,duration,.8,.12)
    for i=1,4 do
        local amount=i/5
        line(p1:Lerp(p3,amount),p2:Lerp(p4,amount),color,duration,.3,.67)
    end
end
local function cone(origin,direction,length,dot,color,duration)
    local d=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)
    if d.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    d=d.Unit
    local side=Vector3.new(-d.Z,0,d.X)
    local half=math.acos(math.clamp(dot,-1,1))
    local center=floorPoint(origin)
    local prev=nil
    for i=0,14 do
        local a=-half+i*half*2/14
        local pt=floorPoint(origin+(d*math.cos(a)+side*math.sin(a))*length)
        if prev then line(prev,pt,color,duration,.64,.13) end
        if i==0 or i==14 or i==7 then line(center,pt,color,duration,.35,.50) end
        prev=pt
    end
end
local function displayWarning(p,duration)
    local shape=p.shape
    local clr=colors.danger
    if shape=="circle" then
        local r=tonumber(p.radius) or 12
        ring(p.origin,r,clr,duration)
        -- Interior outlines indicate this is a filled danger zone, not only an edge.
        if r&gt;=18 then
            ring(p.origin,r*.42,clr,duration,.24,.67)
            ring(p.origin,r*.72,clr,duration,.24,.67)
        end
    elseif shape=="ring" then
        local r=tonumber(p.radius) or 20
        local inside=tonumber(p.innerRadius) or 0
        ring(p.origin,r,clr,duration,.85,.13)
        if inside&gt;0 then ring(p.origin,inside,clr,duration,.55,.27) end
        -- Radial hatching makes the actual annular hit band visible.
        for k=0,7 do
            local a=k*math.pi/4
            local v=Vector3.new(math.cos(a),0,math.sin(a))
            local a0=floorPoint(p.origin+v*math.max(inside,1))
            local a1=floorPoint(p.origin+v*r)
            line(a0,a1,clr,duration,.22,.72)
        end
    elseif shape=="lane" then
        lane(p.origin,p.direction,tonumber(p.range) or 50,tonumber(p.halfWidth) or 5,clr,duration)
    elseif shape=="cone" then
        cone(p.origin,p.direction,tonumber(p.range) or 40,tonumber(p.dot) or .55,clr,duration)
    end
    local pole=Instance.new("Part")
    pole.Name="PhaseAttackWarningAnchor"
    pole.Anchored=true;pole.CanCollide=false;pole.CanQuery=false;pole.CanTouch=false
    pole.Transparency=1;pole.Size=Vector3.one
    pole.Position=p.origin+Vector3.new(0,9,0);pole.Parent=folder
    local billboard=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop=true;billboard.MaxDistance=270
    billboard.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(210,46);billboard.Parent=pole
    local text=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    text.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(65,18,29)
    text.BackgroundTransparency=.16
    text.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,218,171)
    text.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack;text.TextScaled=true
    text.TextStrokeTransparency=.25
    text.Text="⚠ "..tostring(p.name or "COLOSSUS ATTACK")
    text.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1);text.Parent=billboard
    Debris:AddItem(pole,duration+.1)
end
local lastImpactSound=-100
local function playImpact(at,special)
    local now=os.clock()
    if now-lastImpactSound&lt;.11 then return end
    lastImpactSound=now
    local sound=Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.Name="BossPhaseImpactAudio"
    sound.SoundId=(special==11 or special==12) and "rbxasset://sounds/impact_generic.mp3" or "rbxasset://sounds/swordlunge.wav"
    sound.Volume=.9;sound.PlaybackSpeed=.72+rng:NextNumber(0,.22)
    sound.Parent=SoundService;sound:Play()
    Debris:AddItem(sound,2.3)
end
local function announce(text,color,duration)
    local pg=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return end
    local gui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name="BossV18Announcement";gui.IgnoreGuiInset=true;gui.ResetOnSpawn=false
    gui.DisplayOrder=35;gui.Parent=pg
    local title=Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.Name="BossCinematicTitle"
    title.Size=UDim2.new(.88,0,0,85)
    title.Position=UDim2.new(.06,0,.16,0)
    title.BackgroundTransparency=1
    title.TextColor3=color;title.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0)
    title.TextStrokeTransparency=.05
    title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    title.TextScaled=true;title.Text=text
    title.Parent=gui
    TweenService:Create(title,TweenInfo.new(duration*.55,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Position=UDim2.new(.06,0,.11,0)
    }):Play()
    task.delay(duration*.6,function()
        if title.Parent then TweenService:Create(title,TweenInfo.new(duration*.35),{TextTransparency=1,TextStrokeTransparency=1}):Play() end
    end)
    Debris:AddItem(gui,duration+.15)
end
Feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,payload)
    if kind~="Visual" or type(payload)~="table" then return end
    local mode=payload.kind
    if mode~="BossPhaseTell" and mode~="BossPhaseStrike" and mode~="BossRevival"
        and mode~="BossAscended" and mode~="BotTell" and mode~="BotStrike" then return end
    if typeof(payload.origin)~="Vector3" or not near(payload.origin) then return end
    local col=typeof(payload.color)=="Color3" and payload.color or Color3.fromRGB(239,135,249)
    if mode=="BotTell" then
        cone(payload.origin,payload.direction,tonumber(payload.range) or 14,
            tonumber(payload.dot) or .54,colors.danger,
            math.max(.1,tonumber(payload.duration) or .4))
    elseif mode=="BotStrike" then
        orb(payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,2,0),col,.2,6)
        cone(payload.origin,payload.direction,tonumber(payload.range) or 14,
            tonumber(payload.dot) or .54,col,.17)
    elseif mode=="BossRevival" then
        announce("COLOSSUS FALLEN — SECOND WIND",col,3.3)
        ring(payload.origin,12,col,2.4,1.2,.09)
        ring(payload.origin,25,colors.danger,3,1.4,.07)
        orb(payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,8,0),col,3.1,26)
    elseif mode=="BossAscended" then
        announce("ASCENDED COLOSSUS • PHASE II",col,3.0)
        ring(payload.origin,32,col,.55,1.7,.02)
        orb(payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,12,0),col,.9,43)
        playImpact(payload.origin,15)
    elseif mode=="BossPhaseTell" then
        local duration=math.clamp((tonumber(payload.impactServerTime) or Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()+1.4)-Workspace:GetServerTimeNow(),.12,4.2)
        displayWarning(payload,duration)
    elseif mode=="BossPhaseStrike" then
        -- Server issues the strike message immediately before applying damage.
        -- These impact flashes and audio therefore share the authoritative hit moment.
        local shape=payload.shape
        local radius=shape=="ring" and (tonumber(payload.radius) or 22)
            or shape=="circle" and (tonumber(payload.radius) or 13) or 11
        if shape=="lane" then
            lane(payload.origin,payload.direction,tonumber(payload.range) or 60,
                tonumber(payload.halfWidth) or 5,col,.34)
            orb(payload.origin+payload.direction.Unit*math.min(38,tonumber(payload.range) or 38)+Vector3.new(0,2,0),col,.3,7)
        elseif shape=="cone" then
            cone(payload.origin,payload.direction,tonumber(payload.range) or 40,tonumber(payload.dot) or .5,col,.34)
            orb(payload.origin+payload.direction.Unit*22+Vector3.new(0,2,0),col,.3,9)
        else
            ring(payload.origin,radius,col,.48,1.1,.02)
            orb(payload.origin+Vector3.new(0,1.5,0),col,.42,math.min(70,radius*1.05))
        end
        playImpact(payload.origin,tonumber(payload.step) or 6)
    end
end)

          <string name="Name">LashboundV19StatusVisuals</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- One local Highlight + optional light per active status, never hundreds of particles.
local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local colors={
    Slow=Color3.fromRGB(92,175,255),
    Freeze=Color3.fromRGB(182,249,255),
    Burn=Color3.fromRGB(255,106,39),
    Poison=Color3.fromRGB(108,245,91),
    Shock=Color3.fromRGB(255,232,80),
}
local tracked=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local player=Players.LocalPlayer
local tickAt=0
local function strip(model)
    local x=tracked[model]
    if x then
        for _,v in ipairs(x) do if v.Parent then v:Destroy() end end
        tracked[model]=nil
    end
end
local function apply(model,status)
    if not model:IsA("Model") then return end
    local old=tracked[model]
    if old and old[1].Parent and old[1]:GetAttribute("Type")==status then return end
    strip(model)
    local hi=Instance.new("Highlight")
    hi.Name="LashboundElementalGlow"
    hi.FillColor=colors[status]
    hi.OutlineColor=colors[status]
    hi.FillTransparency=.62
    hi.OutlineTransparency=.04
    hi.DepthMode=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
    hi.Adornee=model
    hi:SetAttribute("Type",status)
    hi.Parent=model
    local head=model:FindFirstChild("Head") or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local objects={hi}
    if head then
        local gui=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        gui.Name="StatusLabel"
        gui.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(92,20)
        gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace=Vector3.new(0,4.9,0)
        gui.MaxDistance=100
        gui.AlwaysOnTop=true
        gui.Adornee=head
        gui.Parent=gui.Parent or head
        local text=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        text.BackgroundTransparency=1
        text.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
        text.Text=status:upper()
        text.TextColor3=colors[status]
        text.TextStrokeTransparency=.1
        text.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        text.TextScaled=true
        text.Parent=gui
        table.insert(objects,gui)
    end
    tracked[model]=objects
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if os.clock()-tickAt&lt;.22 then return end
    tickAt=os.clock()
    local root=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    local models={}
    for _,p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Character then table.insert(models,p.Character) end
    end
    local arena=Workspace:FindFirstChild("LashboundBattleArena")
    if arena then
        for _,folder in ipairs(arena:GetChildren()) do
            if folder.Name=="LashboundBots" or folder.Name=="LashboundBosses" then
                for _,model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") then table.insert(models,model) end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local seen={}
    for _,model in ipairs(models) do
        seen[model]=true
        local part=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local status=model:GetAttribute("LashboundStatus")
        local expiry=model:GetAttribute("LashboundStatusUntil") or 0
        if part and (root.Position-part.Position).Magnitude&lt;160 and colors[status]
            and expiry&gt;Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() then
            apply(model,status)
        else
            strip(model)
        end
    end
    for model in pairs(tracked) do if not seen[model] then strip(model) end end
end)

 gui.DisplayOrder=28;gui.Enabled=false;gui.Parent=me:WaitForChild('PlayerGui')

info.TextWrapped=true

-- Orthogonal bird's-eye composition, looking straight down the Y axis.
-- A WorldModel presents real 3D silhouettes, avoiding unsupported chess fonts.
local boardFrame=Instance.new('Frame')

local world=Instance.new('WorldModel');world.Name='TabletopScene';world.Parent=viewport
local tiles=Instance.new('Folder');tiles.Name='ChessSquareModels';tiles.Parent=world

local boardEdge=Instance.new('Part')

local board={}
local state=nil
local selected=nil
local frames={}
local shownPieces={}
local flatPieces={}
local visibleBoard={}
local shownVersion=-1
local shownMode=''
local glyphLetters={k='K',q='Q',r='R',b='B',n='N',p='P'}
local function sqCFrame(index)
    local row=math.floor((index-1)/8)+1
    local col=(index-1)%8+1
    return CFrame.new((col-4.5)*Factory.Square,.03,(row-4.5)*Factory.Square)
end
local function removeSquare(i)
    local model=shownPieces[i]
    if model then model:Destroy() end
    shownPieces[i]=nil
end
local function addSquare(i,piece)
    if not piece or piece=='' then return end
    local model=Factory.Create(piece,visualFolder,sqCFrame(i))
    model:SetAttribute('Square',i)
    shownPieces[i]=model
end
local function fullSync(b)
    visualFolder:ClearAllChildren()
    shownPieces={}
    for i=1,64 do
        if b[i] and b[i]~='' then addSquare(i,b[i]) end
    end
end
local function repairPieces(b)
    for i=1,64 do
        local want=b[i]
        local model=shownPieces[i]
        if not want or want=='' then
            if model then removeSquare(i) end
        elseif not model or model:GetAttribute('ChessPiece')~=want then
            if model then removeSquare(i) end
            addSquare(i,want)
        end
    end
end
local function animateMove(model,i)
    if not model or not model.PrimaryPart then return end
    TweenService:Create(model.PrimaryPart,
        TweenInfo.new(MOVE_TIME,Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
        {CFrame=sqCFrame(i)}):Play()
end
local function applyViewState(data)
    local b=data.board or {}
    if data.version==shownVersion then return end
    local move=data.lastMove
    local from=type(move)=='table' and move[1]
    local to=type(move)=='table' and move[2]
    local simpleMove=type(from)=='number' and type(to)=='number'
        and data.version==shownVersion+1 and shownPieces[from]~=nil
        and (data.mode=='pvp' or data.mode=='bot')
    if not simpleMove then
        fullSync(b)
    else
        local moving=shownPieces[from]
        removeSquare(to) -- captured piece leaves immediately
        shownPieces[from]=nil
        shownPieces[to]=moving
        moving:SetAttribute('Square',to)
        animateMove(moving,to)
        -- If a king castles, move the rook at the same instant.
        local prevPiece=moving:GetAttribute('ChessPiece')
        if prevPiece and prevPiece:lower()=='k' and math.abs(to-from)==2 then
            local r=math.floor((from-1)/8)+1
            local kingSide=to&gt;from
            local rookFrom=(r-1)*8+(kingSide and 8 or 1)
            local rookTo=(r-1)*8+(kingSide and 6 or 4)
            local rook=shownPieces[rookFrom]
            if rook then
                shownPieces[rookFrom]=nil;shownPieces[rookTo]=rook
                rook:SetAttribute('Square',rookTo)
                animateMove(rook,rookTo)
            end
        end
        local version=data.version
        task.delay(MOVE_TIME+.02,function()
            if shownVersion==version and gui.Enabled then repairPieces(b) end
        end)
    end
    -- Keep the displayed pieces and authoritative board state synchronized.
    -- Chess Unicode glyphs can render blank in a font fallback on some devices.
    -- All six types now have legible letter marks on distinct team-colored tokens.
    for i=1,64 do
        local token=flatPieces[i]
        if token then
            local piece=b[i] or ''
            local occupied=type(piece)=='string' and piece~=''
            token.Visible=occupied
            if occupied then
                local white=piece:match('%u')~=nil
                token.BackgroundColor3=white and Color3.fromRGB(249,245,227) or Color3.fromRGB(37,44,42)
                local rim=token:FindFirstChildOfClass('UIStroke')
                if rim then rim.Color=white and Color3.fromRGB(82,80,69) or Color3.fromRGB(237,239,222) end
                local mark=token:FindFirstChild('PieceLetter')
                if mark then
                    mark.Text=piece:upper() -- K Q R B N P always exist in GothamBlack
                    mark.TextColor3=white and Color3.fromRGB(40,48,43) or Color3.fromRGB(251,251,239)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    shownVersion=data.version
    shownMode=data.mode
end

-- Transparent 8x8 click grid OVER the rendered 3D pieces.
-- White's home row is on the bottom and black's home row at the top.
local function highlight()
    for ix,tile in ipairs(frames) do
        local last=state and state.lastMove
        local active=selected==ix
        local recent=last and (last[1]==ix or last[2]==ix)
        local row=math.floor((ix-1)/8)+1
        local col=(ix-1)%8+1
        tile.BackgroundColor3=active and Color3.fromRGB(246,246,105) or recent and Color3.fromRGB(202,211,104) or ((row+col)%2==0 and Color3.fromRGB(238,238,210) or Color3.fromRGB(118,150,86))
        tile.BackgroundTransparency=0
    end
end
for row=1,8 do for col=1,8 do
    local i=(row-1)*8+col
    -- There is a small 6px border around the 3D tiles; each tile is 51.5px.
    local tile=button(boardFrame,'',UDim2.fromOffset(6+(col-1)*51.5,6+(row-1)*51.5),
        UDim2.fromOffset(51.5,51.5),Color3.fromRGB(245,212,115))
    tile.Name='Square_'..i
    tile.BackgroundTransparency=1;tile.Text=''
    tile.AutoButtonColor=false;tile.ZIndex=3
    frames[i]=tile
    tile.BackgroundColor3=(row+col)%2==0 and Color3.fromRGB(238,238,210) or Color3.fromRGB(118,150,86)
    tile.BackgroundTransparency=0
    local token=Instance.new('Frame')
    token.Name='ChessPieceToken'
    token.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,.5)
    token.Position=UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
    token.Size=UDim2.fromScale(.76,.76)
    token.BorderSizePixel=0
    token.ZIndex=4
    token.Visible=false
    token.Parent=tile
    local round=Instance.new('UICorner')
    round.CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    round.Parent=token
    local rim=Instance.new('UIStroke')
    rim.Thickness=2
    rim.Parent=token
    local mark=Instance.new('TextLabel')
    mark.Name='PieceLetter'
    mark.BackgroundTransparency=1
    mark.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
    mark.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    mark.TextScaled=true
    mark.Text=''
    mark.ZIndex=5
    mark.Parent=token
    flatPieces[i]=token
    local tiny=label(tile,'',UDim2.fromOffset(34,35),UDim2.fromOffset(15,13),Enum.Font.GothamBold,Color3.fromRGB(102,87,72))
    tiny.Name='RoleHint'
    tiny.TextStrokeTransparency=0
    tile.Activated:Connect(function()
        if not state or state.ended then return end
        local ownColor=me.UserId==state.white and 'w' or me.UserId==state.black and 'b' or ''
        if ownColor~=state.turn then return end
        local piece=board[i]
        local friendly=piece and piece~='' and ((piece:match('%u')~=nil and ownColor=='w') or (piece:match('%l')~=nil and ownColor=='b'))
        if selected and selected~=i then
            if friendly then selected=i
            else Request:FireServer('Move',selected,i);selected=nil end
        elseif friendly then selected=i
        else selected=nil end
        highlight()
    end)
end end
local message=label(panel,'WHITE AT BOTTOM  ·  SELECT PIECE → DESTINATION  ·  LIVE TABLE SYNC',
    UDim2.fromOffset(14,559),UDim2.fromOffset(468,27),Enum.Font.Gotham,Color3.fromRGB(227,206,169))
message.TextSize=13;message.TextScaled=false
local function refresh(data)
    if type(data)~='table' or type(data.board)~='table' then return end
    local previousVersion=state and state.version or -1
    state=data
    board=data.board
    selected=nil
    if data.mode=='idle' then
        info.Text='Choose 2 players or bot to begin.'
    else
        local identity=me.UserId==data.white and 'WHITE' or me.UserId==data.black and 'BLACK' or 'SPECTATOR'
        info.Text=identity..'  ·  '..tostring(data.status or '')
    end
    if gui.Enabled then applyViewState(data) end
    for i,tile in ipairs(frames) do
        local piece=board[i]
        local hint=tile:FindFirstChild('RoleHint')
        if hint then
            hint.Text=(piece and piece~='') and (glyphLetters[piece:lower()] or '') or ''
            hint.TextColor3=(piece and piece:match('%u')) and Color3.fromRGB(251,242,215) or Color3.fromRGB(40,29,32)
        end
    end
    highlight()
end
local function open()
    gui.Enabled=true;selected=nil;shownVersion=-1
    Request:FireServer('View')
end
local function hide()
    gui.Enabled=false;selected=nil;highlight()
end
close.Activated:Connect(hide)
versus.Activated:Connect(function()selected=nil;Request:FireServer('PvP')end)
bot.Activated:Connect(function()selected=nil;Request:FireServer('Bot')end)
resign.Activated:Connect(function()selected=nil;Request:FireServer('Resign')end)
-- Only one chess table exists: never block waiting for removed remotes 2-4.
State.OnClientEvent:Connect(refresh)
Feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind)
    if kind=='OpenChess' then
        shownVersion=-1
        open()
    end
end)
local function resize()
    local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end
    local size=camera.ViewportSize
    scale.Scale=math.min(1,(size.X-12)/496,(size.Y-12)/602)
end
resize()
workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal('CurrentCamera'):Connect(resize)
if workspace.CurrentCamera then workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal('ViewportSize'):Connect(resize) end
me:GetAttributeChangedSignal('Zone'):Connect(function()
    if me:GetAttribute('Zone')=='Arena' then hide() end
end)
