local function makePart(pos,color,size,alpha)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanQuery=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CastShadow=false
    p.Material=Enum.Material.Neon;p.Color=color;p.Transparency=alpha or .1
    p.Size=size;p.CFrame=CFrame.new(pos);p.Parent=fx
    return p
end
local function line(a,b,color,duration,width)
    local d=b-a
    if d.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    local p=makePart((a+b)*.5,color,Vector3.new(width or .35,width or .35,d.Magnitude),.02)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)*.5,b)
    Tween:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(duration),{Transparency=1}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(p,duration+.1)
end
local function ring(pos,color,size,duration)
    local last=nil
    for j=0,12 do
        local a=math.pi*2*j/12
        local point=pos+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*size,.55,math.sin(a)*size)
        if last then line(last,point,color,duration,.28) end
        last=point
    end
end
local function sound()
    local s=Instance.new("Sound")
    local asset=ids.ThunderVerdict
    s.SoundId=(type(asset)=="number" and asset&gt;0) and ("rbxassetid://"..asset)
        or "rbxasset://sounds/electronicpingshort.wav"
    s.Volume=1.2;s.PlaybackSpeed=.85
    s.Parent=SoundService;s:Play();Debris:AddItem(s,3)
    local bass=Instance.new("Sound")
    bass.SoundId="rbxasset://sounds/impact_generic.mp3"
    bass.Volume=.65;bass.PlaybackSpeed=.75;bass.Parent=SoundService
    bass:Play();Debris:AddItem(bass,2)
end
local function nearby(pos)
    local root=me.Character and me.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return root and (root.Position-pos).Magnitude&lt;175
end
Feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,payload)
    if kind~="Visual" or type(payload)~="table" then return end
    if payload.kind~="JudgementLock" and payload.kind~="JudgementStrike" then return end
    local pos=payload.origin
    if typeof(pos)~="Vector3" or not nearby(pos) then return end
    local gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,241,112)
    local cyan=Color3.fromRGB(122,227,255)
    if payload.kind=="JudgementLock" then
        for i=1,3 do
            ring(pos,gold,3.4+i*.65,1.03)
            ring(pos,cyan,1.5+i*.6,1.03)
        end
        local start=pos+Vector3.new(0,1.5,0)
        line(start,start+Vector3.new(0,15,0),cyan,.8,.2)
    else
        sound()
        local start=pos+Vector3.new(0,34,0)
        local prev=start
        for i=1,13 do
            local fraction=i/13
            local nextPoint=pos+Vector3.new((i==13 and 0 or rng:NextNumber(-2.8,2.8)),34*(1-fraction),
                (i==13 and 0 or rng:NextNumber(-2.8,2.8)))
            line(prev,nextPoint,i%3==0 and cyan or gold,.35,i%3==0 and .65 or .44)
            prev=nextPoint
        end
        local flash=makePart(pos+Vector3.new(0,3,0),gold,Vector3.new(2,2,2),.02)
        flash.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
        Tween:Create(flash,TweenInfo.new(.38,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart),{Size=Vector3.new(12,12,12),Transparency=1}):Play()
        Debris:AddItem(flash,.42)
        ring(pos,gold,9,.43)
        ring(pos,cyan,5,.27)
    end
end)

local function clickable()
    return snap and snap.side&gt;0 and snap.turn==snap.side and (snap.mode=="bot" or snap.mode=="pvp")
        and snap.winner==0 and not snap.busy
end
local function addConnectFour()
    local board=snap.board or {}
    local holder=panel(body,UDim2.new(.94,0,.93,0),UDim2.new(.03,0,.04,0),Color3.fromRGB(32,67,157))
    local grid=create(holder,"UIGridLayout",{CellSize=UDim2.new(1/7,-6,1/6,-5),CellPadding=UDim2.fromOffset(3,3),
        SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
        VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center})
    for row=6,1,-1 do
        for col=1,7 do
            local value=board[(row-1)*7+col] or 0
            local disk=create(holder,"TextButton",{Name="Column"..col,LayoutOrder=(6-row)*7+col,Text="",
                BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(27,60,137),BorderSizePixel=0})
            create(disk,"UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)})
            local ball=create(disk,"Frame",{Size=UDim2.fromScale(.84,.84),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,.5),
                Position=UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5),BorderSizePixel=0,
                BackgroundColor3=value==1 and Color3.fromRGB(246,81,62) or
                    value==2 and Color3.fromRGB(255,222,72) or Color3.fromRGB(218,231,237)})
            create(ball,"UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)})
            local index=(row-1)*7+col
            if value&gt;0 and (previousFour[index] or 0)==0 then
                ball.Position=UDim2.fromScale(.5,-3.2)
                TweenService:Create(ball,TweenInfo.new(.36,Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
                    Position=UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)}):Play()
            end
            disk.Activated:Connect(function()
                if clickable() then Request:FireServer(current,"Move",col) end
            end)
        end
    end
    previousFour=table.clone(board)
    tip.Text="Tap a column to drop a disc. Four in a row wins!"
end
local function miniGrid(parent,originX,text,colorSet,clickableEnemy)
    local labelText=label(parent,text,UDim2.new(.46,0,0,24),UDim2.new(originX,0,0,0),gold,17)
    local boardFrame=panel(parent,UDim2.new(.46,0,.84,0),UDim2.new(originX,0,.13,0),Color3.fromRGB(38,62,88))
    local layout=create(boardFrame,"UIGridLayout",{CellSize=UDim2.new(.1,-2,.1,-2),CellPadding=UDim2.fromOffset(1,1),
        HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder})
    for i=1,100 do
        local state=colorSet[i] or 0
        local fill=state==1 and Color3.fromRGB(210,211,219) or state==2 and Color3.fromRGB(233,77,64) or
            state==3 and Color3.fromRGB(135,176,183) or Color3.fromRGB(76,116,149)
        local square=create(boardFrame,"TextButton",{Text=state==2 and "X" or state==1 and "·" or "",
            LayoutOrder=i,BackgroundColor3=fill,TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),TextScaled=true,Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold,BorderSizePixel=0})
        square.Activated:Connect(function()
            if clickableEnemy and clickable() and state==0 then Request:FireServer(current,"Move",i) end
        end)
    end
end
local function addBattleship()
    local enemy=snap.enemyMarks or {}
    local incoming=snap.incomingMarks or {}
    local fleet=snap.ownFleet or {}
    local own={}
    for i=1,100 do
        if incoming[i]==2 then own[i]=2
        elseif incoming[i]==1 then own[i]=1
        elseif fleet[i]==1 then own[i]=3
        else own[i]=0 end
    end
    miniGrid(body,.02,"YOUR FLEET",own,false)
    miniGrid(body,.52,"FIRE HERE",enemy,true)
    tip.Text="Blue = ocean · gray = miss · red = hit · muted blue = your ship"
end
local function poolXY(b)
    return UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp((b.x+.91)/1.82,.03,.97),math.clamp((b.z+.43)/.86,.04,.96))
end
local function updatePool()
    if not poolView or not poolView.felt.Parent then return end
    for _,b in ipairs(snap.balls or {}) do
        local ball=poolView.balls[b.n]
        if ball then
            ball.Visible=b.active
            if b.active then
                TweenService:Create(ball,TweenInfo.new(.095,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),{Position=poolXY(b)}):Play()
            end
        end
    end
    if poolView.guide then
        local cue=(snap.balls or {})[1]
        poolView.guide.Visible=cue and cue.active and not snap.busy or false
        if cue then
            poolView.guide.Position=poolXY(cue)
            poolView.guide.Rotation=angle
        end
    end
    poolView.turn.Text=string.format("AIM %d°    POWER %d%%    SCORE %d : %d",angle,power,
        (snap.score or {})[1] or 0,(snap.score or {})[2] or 0)
    tip.Text=snap.busy and "SHOT IN MOTION — WATCH THE BALLS ROLL" or
        "Line up your cue, adjust power, and SHOOT. Pocketed balls score points."
end
local function addPool()
    local felt=panel(body,UDim2.new(.93,0,.68,0),UDim2.new(.035,0,.02,0),Color3.fromRGB(20,107,77))
    create(felt,"UIStroke",{Thickness=5,Color=Color3.fromRGB(104,57,35)})
    for _,pocketLocation in ipairs({{0,0},{.5,0},{1,0},{0,1},{.5,1},{1,1}}) do
        local pocket=create(felt,"Frame",{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(16,16),Position=UDim2.fromScale(pocketLocation[1],pocketLocation[2]),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,.5),
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,10,10),BorderSizePixel=0})
        create(pocket,"UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)})
    end
    local guide=create(felt,"Frame",{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(80,2),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,.5),
        Position=UDim2.fromScale(.25,.5),BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(246,244,218),BackgroundTransparency=.28,
        BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=3})
    local balls={}
    for _,b in ipairs(snap.balls or {}) do
        local ball=create(felt,"TextLabel",{Name="Ball"..b.n,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(16,16),AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,.5),
            Position=poolXY(b),Visible=b.active,
            Text=b.n==0 and "" or tostring(b.n),TextSize=10,Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BackgroundColor3=b.n==0 and Color3.new(1,1,1) or b.n==8 and Color3.fromRGB(26,27,32) or
                (b.n%2==0 and Color3.fromRGB(212,80,76) or Color3.fromRGB(244,201,68)),BorderSizePixel=0,ZIndex=5})
        create(ball,"UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)})
        balls[b.n]=ball
    end
    local ctrl=panel(body,UDim2.new(.97,0,.24,0),UDim2.new(.015,0,.75,0),Color3.fromRGB(57,44,35))
    local function ctr(text,x,fn)
        local b=create(ctrl,"TextButton",{Text=text,Size=UDim2.new(.15,-3,.65,0),Position=UDim2.new(x,0,.15,0),
            BackgroundColor3=wood,TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold,TextScaled=true,BorderSizePixel=0})
        corners(b,6);b.Activated:Connect(fn)
    end
    ctr("ANGLE -",.01,function() angle=(angle-10)%360;updatePool() end)
    ctr("ANGLE +",.17,function() angle=(angle+10)%360;updatePool() end)
    ctr("POWER -",.34,function() power=math.max(10,power-10);updatePool() end)
    ctr("POWER +",.50,function() power=math.min(100,power+10);updatePool() end)
    ctr("SHOOT",.70,function() if clickable() then Request:FireServer(current,"Move",{angle=angle,power=power}) end end)
    local turn=label(body,"",UDim2.new(.95,0,0,22),UDim2.new(.03,0,.72,0),gold,16)
    poolView={felt=felt,balls=balls,guide=guide,turn=turn}
    updatePool()
end
local function render()
    if not snap then return end
    title.Text=(mapping[current] or "CABIN GAME ROOM").."  ·  PLAYER "..tostring(snap.side)
    local statusText=snap.mode=="idle" and "Choose Bot or Host PvP." or
        snap.mode=="waiting" and "Waiting for a second player — press JOIN on their screen." or
        snap.winner&gt;0 and (snap.winner==3 and "DRAW!" or "PLAYER "..tostring(snap.winner).." WINS!") or
        snap.busy and "Balls moving — watch the table!" or
        ("Turn: "..(snap.turn==1 and "Player 1" or "Player 2").." · "..(snap.notice or ""))
    status.Text=statusText
    if snap.mode=="idle" then
        tip.Text="START HERE: tap VS BOT for a solo game, or HOST PvP and invite a friend to JOIN."
        tip.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,223,142)
    elseif snap.mode=="waiting" then
        tip.Text="Second player: walk to this table and press JOIN PvP."
    else
        tip.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(213,205,194)
    end
    bot.Visible=snap.mode=="idle" or snap.winner~=0
    host.Visible=snap.mode=="idle" or snap.winner~=0
    join.Visible=snap.mode=="waiting" and snap.side==0
    restart.Visible=snap.winner~=0 and snap.side~=0
    local isPool=current=="PoolA"
    if isPool and poolView and poolView.felt.Parent then
        updatePool() -- preserve UI and tween moving balls, don't rebuild at every snapshot
        return
    end
    for _,ch in ipairs(body:GetChildren()) do ch:Destroy() end
    poolView=nil
    if current=="ConnectFour" then addConnectFour()
    elseif current=="Battleship" then addBattleship()
    elseif isPool then addPool()
    end
    if snap.mode=="idle" then
        tip.Text="Choose VS BOT or HOST PvP at the top to begin."
    end
end
State.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(key,verb,data)
    if verb=="Close" and key==current then ui.Enabled=false;return end
    if verb=="Open" then
        if current~=key then poolView=nil;previousFour={} end
        current=key;snap=data;ui.Enabled=true;render()
    elseif key==current and ui.Enabled then
        local previousVersion=lastVersion
        lastVersion=data.version or -1
        if current~="PoolA" and lastVersion~=previousVersion then
            boardTick(data.winner and data.winner&gt;0)
        end
        snap=data;render()
    end
end)
-- Fit narrow mobile viewports without relying on the main combat HUD layout.
local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera
local scale=create(frame,"UIScale",{Scale=1})
local function resize()
    local c=workspace.CurrentCamera
    if c then
        local v=c.ViewportSize
        scale.Scale=math.clamp(math.min(v.X/810,v.Y/700),.5,1)
        frame.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,.5)
        frame.Position=UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
    end
end
resize()
if cam then cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(resize) end

-- Uses Roblox-stock particle textures; uploaded audio assets are optional.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local rng=Random.new()

local names={
    [6]="BossStarLance",[7]="BossMeteor",[8]="BossTrident",[9]="BossCrystal",

local function near(p)
    local c=player.Character
    local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return r and (r.Position-p).Magnitude&lt;360
end
local function simple(name,pos,sz,color,mat,alpha,shape)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name=name;p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CanQuery=false
    p.CastShadow=false;p.Material=mat or Enum.Material.Neon;p.Color=color
    p.Transparency=alpha or 0;p.Size=sz;p.CFrame=CFrame.new(pos)
    if shape then p.Shape=shape end
    p.Parent=fx
    return p
end
local function burst(p,color,count,speed,lifetime,smoke)
    local holder=simple("BossTextureAnchor",p,Vector3.new(.2,.2,.2),color,Enum.Material.Neon,1)
    local emitter=Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    emitter.Texture=smoke and SMOKE or PARTICLE
    emitter.Rate=0;emitter.Lifetime=NumberRange.new(lifetime*.45,lifetime)
    emitter.Speed=NumberRange.new(speed*.5,speed)
    emitter.SpreadAngle=Vector2.new(150,150)
    emitter.Drag=4
    emitter.LightEmission=smoke and .15 or .85
    emitter.Color=ColorSequence.new(color,Color3.new(1,1,1))
    emitter.Size=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,smoke and 5 or 1.8),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0)})
    emitter.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,.1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1)})
    emitter.Parent=holder;emitter:Emit(count)
    Debris:AddItem(holder,lifetime+1)
end
local function disc(center,radius,color,life)
    local cyl=simple("CinematicShockDisc",center,Vector3.new(.35,1.4,1.4),color,Enum.Material.Neon,.58,Enum.PartType.Cylinder)
    cyl.CFrame=CFrame.new(center)*CFrame.Angles(0,0,math.pi/2)
    Tween:Create(cyl,TweenInfo.new(life,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad),{
        Size=Vector3.new(.2,radius*2,radius*2),Transparency=1}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(cyl,life+.05)
end
local function shaft(origin,direction,length,color,life,width)
    local d=direction.Magnitude&gt;.01 and direction.Unit or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
    local center=origin+d*length*.5
    local part=simple("VolumetricBossBeam",center,Vector3.new(width,width,length),color,Enum.Material.Neon,.32)
    part.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(center,origin+d*length)
    Tween:Create(part,TweenInfo.new(life,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad),{
        Size=Vector3.new(.12,.12,length),Transparency=1}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(part,life+.08)
    burst(center,color,10,13,.7)
end
local function pillars(origin,color,count,height,spread,life)
    for i=1,count do
        local a=math.pi*2*i/count
        local r=spread*(.65+.35*(i%3)/2)
        local p=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*r,height*.5,math.sin(a)*r)
        local pillar=simple("AscensionLightningPillar",p,Vector3.new(.8,height,.8),color,Enum.Material.Neon,.22)
        pillar.CFrame=pillar.CFrame*CFrame.Angles(0,0,(i%2==0 and 1 or -1)*.1)
        Tween:Create(pillar,TweenInfo.new(life),{Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(.12,height,.12)}):Play()
        Debris:AddItem(pillar,life+.1)
    end
end
local function soundAt(index,pos)
    local soundSpec=soundPalette[index] or soundPalette[1]
    local customKey=names[index]
    local asset=customKey and SoundIds[customKey]
    local s=Instance.new("Sound")
    s.Name="ColossusDistinct_"..tostring(index)
    s.SoundId=type(asset)=="number" and asset&gt;0 and "rbxassetid://"..asset or soundSpec[1]
    s.Volume=.82;s.PlaybackSpeed=soundSpec[2]
    s.RollOffMaxDistance=300
    local emitter=simple("BossAudioPoint",pos,Vector3.new(.1,.1,.1),Color3.new(1,1,1),Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,1)
    s.Parent=emitter;s:Play()
    Debris:AddItem(emitter,3)
end
-- Every 3D effect below is generated from the same metadata the server uses
-- for its strike. It is purely cosmetic and cannot expand a damage zone.
local function connectPoints(a,b,color,life,thick,alpha)
    local d=b-a
    if d.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    local beam=simple("BossVolumetricRib",(a+b)*.5,Vector3.new(thick or .45,thick or .45,d.Magnitude),color,Enum.Material.Neon,alpha or .24)
    beam.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)*.5,b)
    Tween:Create(beam,TweenInfo.new(life),{Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(.06,.06,d.Magnitude)}):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(beam,life+.08)
end
local function ring3d(origin,inner,outer,color,life)
    local slices=20
    local low=math.max(0,inner or 0)
    for j=0,slices-1 do
        local a=j*math.pi*2/slices
        local b=(j+1)*math.pi*2/slices
        for _,radius in ipairs({outer}) do
            local first=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*radius,2.3,math.sin(a)*radius)
            local last=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(b)*radius,2.3,math.sin(b)*radius)
            connectPoints(first,last,color,life,.6,.25)
        end
        if j%2==0 and low&gt;0 then
            local pa=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*low,2.3,math.sin(a)*low)
            local pb=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*outer,2.3,math.sin(a)*outer)
            connectPoints(pa,pb,color,life,.22,.64)
        end
    end
    -- Volumetric wave fronts rise above the floor instead of lying flat.
    -- They use the same inner/outer radii as the authoritative ring strike.
    for j=0,slices-1,2 do
        local a=j*math.pi*2/slices
        local q=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*outer,2.3,math.sin(a)*outer)
        connectPoints(q,q+Vector3.new(0,5.0,0),color,life,.40,.56)
        if low&gt;0 then
            local innerQ=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*low,2.3,math.sin(a)*low)
            connectPoints(innerQ,innerQ+Vector3.new(0,2.0,0),color,life,.20,.68)
        end
    end
    if low&gt;0 then
        for j=0,slices-1,2 do
            local a=j*math.pi*2/slices
            local b=(j+2)*math.pi*2/slices
            connectPoints(origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*low,1.9,math.sin(a)*low),
                origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(b)*low,1.9,math.sin(b)*low),color,life,.25,.45)
        end
    end
end
local function lane3d(origin,direction,length,halfWidth,color,life)
    local d=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)
    if d.Magnitude&lt;.01 then return end
    d=d.Unit
    local right=Vector3.new(-d.Z,0,d.X)
    local lengthSafe=math.clamp(length,1,180)
    local widthSafe=math.clamp(halfWidth,1,20)
    -- Twin lane walls span precisely the server's +/- halfWidth.
    for _,sgn in ipairs({-1,1}) do
        local mid=origin+d*lengthSafe*.5+right*sgn*widthSafe+Vector3.new(0,3.5,0)
        local wall=simple("BossDamageLaneBoundary",mid,Vector3.new(.25,6.5,lengthSafe),color,Enum.Material.Neon,.63)
        wall.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(mid,mid+d)
        Tween:Create(wall,TweenInfo.new(life),{Transparency=1}):Play()
        Debris:AddItem(wall,life+.06)
    end
    shaft(origin+Vector3.new(0,3.2,0),d,lengthSafe,color,life,math.min(widthSafe*1.4,12))
    for k=1,5 do
        local pos=origin+d*(lengthSafe*k/6)+Vector3.new(0,3.1,0)
        burst(pos,color,4,12,.45)
    end
end
local function cone3d(origin,direction,length,dot,color,life)
    local d=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)
    if d.Magnitude&lt;.01 then return end
    d=d.Unit
    local side=Vector3.new(-d.Z,0,d.X)
    local angle=math.acos(math.clamp(dot,-1,1))
    local radius=math.clamp(length,2,120)
    local prev=nil
    for i=0,16 do
        local a=-angle+(2*angle*i/16)
        local q=origin+(d*math.cos(a)+side*math.sin(a))*radius+Vector3.new(0,2.8,0)
        if prev then connectPoints(prev,q,color,life,.44,.2) end
        if i%4==0 then connectPoints(origin+Vector3.new(0,2.8,0),q,color,life,.30,.6) end
        prev=q
    end
    -- A restrained cone of vertical energy spines follows the visible boundary.
    for k=0,6 do
        local a=-angle+(2*angle*k/6)
        local q=origin+(d*math.cos(a)+side*math.sin(a))*radius+Vector3.new(0,2.8,0)
        connectPoints(q,q+Vector3.new(0,5,0),color,life,.26,.65)
    end
end
local function onStrike(p)
    local origin=p.origin
    local col=typeof(p.color)=="Color3" and p.color or Color3.fromRGB(248,117,92)
    local idx=tonumber(p.step) or 1
    local shape=p.shape
    local life=.55
    if shape=="lane" then
        lane3d(origin,typeof(p.direction)=="Vector3" and p.direction or Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
            tonumber(p.range) or 50,tonumber(p.halfWidth) or 5,col,life)
    elseif shape=="ring" then
        local radius=tonumber(p.radius) or 30
        local inner=tonumber(p.innerRadius) or 0
        ring3d(origin,inner,radius,col,.68)
        -- Never fill the inner safe pocket with a misleading full disc.
    elseif shape=="circle" then
        local radius=tonumber(p.radius) or 18
        ring3d(origin,0,radius,col,life)
        local dome=simple("BossImpactDome",origin+Vector3.new(0,3.5,0),Vector3.new(3,3,3),col,Enum.Material.Glass,.58,Enum.PartType.Ball)
        Tween:Create(dome,TweenInfo.new(.46),{Size=Vector3.new(radius*1.65,math.max(6,radius*.8),radius*1.65),Transparency=1}):Play()
        Debris:AddItem(dome,.5)
    elseif shape=="cone" then
        cone3d(origin,typeof(p.direction)=="Vector3" and p.direction or Vector3.new(0,0,-1),
            tonumber(p.range) or 45,tonumber(p.dot) or .35,col,life)
    else
        ring3d(origin,0,22,col,.4)
    end
    burst(origin+Vector3.new(0,3,0),col,idx&gt;=6 and 18 or 12,22,.75)
    -- Materialized strike core, never collidable and removed before the next attack.
    local core=simple("BossVolumetricImpactCore",origin+Vector3.new(0,3,0),Vector3.new(3,3,3),col,Enum.Material.Glass,.35,Enum.PartType.Ball)
    Tween:Create(core,TweenInfo.new(.32,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Size=Vector3.new(idx&gt;=6 and 13 or 8,idx&gt;=6 and 12 or 7,idx&gt;=6 and 13 or 8),Transparency=1
    }):Play()
    Debris:AddItem(core,.37)
    soundAt(idx,origin)
end
-- All devices receive restrained camera vibration; supported controllers also
-- rumble via HapticService. Server-impact events trigger both, not warnings.
local impactShakeToken=0
local rumbleToken=0
local function vibrate(origin,power,duration)
    local c=player.Character
    local hum=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not r or not hum then return end
    local distance=(r.Position-origin).Magnitude
    local intensity=math.clamp(power*(1-distance/270),0,.90)
    if intensity&lt;.035 then return end
    impactShakeToken+=1
    rumbleToken+=1
    local shakeID=impactShakeToken
    local rumbleID=rumbleToken
    local base=hum.CameraOffset
    local window=math.clamp(duration,.12,.8)
    task.spawn(function()
        local start=os.clock()
        while os.clock()-start&lt;window and shakeID==impactShakeToken and hum.Parent do
            local fraction=1-(os.clock()-start)/window
            local amount=intensity*fraction*.72
            hum.CameraOffset=base+Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-amount,amount),rng:NextNumber(-amount,amount),0)
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
        if shakeID==impactShakeToken and hum.Parent then hum.CameraOffset=base end
    end)
    local gamepad=Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
    local large=Enum.VibrationMotor.Large
    local small=Enum.VibrationMotor.Small
    local supported=false
    pcall(function()
        supported=HapticService:IsVibrationSupported(gamepad) and HapticService:IsMotorSupported(gamepad,large)
        if supported then
            HapticService:SetMotor(gamepad,large,intensity)
            if HapticService:IsMotorSupported(gamepad,small) then
                HapticService:SetMotor(gamepad,small,math.min(1,intensity*.75))
            end
        end
    end)
    if supported then
        task.delay(window,function()
            if rumbleID~=rumbleToken then return end
            pcall(function()
                HapticService:SetMotor(gamepad,large,0)
                if HapticService:IsMotorSupported(gamepad,small) then HapticService:SetMotor(gamepad,small,0) end
            end)
        end)
    end
end

local shakeToken=0
local function revive(p)
    shakeToken+=1
    local token=shakeToken
    local origin=p.origin
    local color=typeof(p.color)=="Color3" and p.color or Color3.fromRGB(250,173,137)
    local tint=Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
    tint.Name="V22BossReviveFlash"
    tint.TintColor=Color3.new(1,1,1)
    tint.Contrast=0;tint.Saturation=0
    tint.Parent=Lighting
    Tween:Create(tint,TweenInfo.new(.45),{TintColor=color,Contrast=.35,Saturation=-.3}):Play()
    task.delay(2.4,function()
        if tint.Parent then Tween:Create(tint,TweenInfo.new(1.1),{TintColor=Color3.new(1,1,1),Contrast=0,Saturation=0}):Play() end
    end)
    Debris:AddItem(tint,(tonumber(p.duration) or 5.1)+.2)
    burst(origin+Vector3.new(0,7,0),color,58,28,2.7)
    burst(origin+Vector3.new(0,7,0),Color3.fromRGB(65,57,68),26,12,2.9,true)
    pillars(origin,color,11,35,18,2.7)
    for i=1,9 do
        task.delay((i-1)*.41,function()
            if token~=shakeToken then return end
            disc(origin+Vector3.new(0,i*.65,0),13+i*8,color,1.4)
        end)
    end
    local hum=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local offset=hum and hum.CameraOffset or Vector3.zero
    task.spawn(function()
        for i=1,45 do
            if token~=shakeToken or not hum or not hum.Parent then break end
            local scale=(1-i/46)*.24
            hum.CameraOffset=offset+Vector3.new(rng:NextNumber(-scale,scale),rng:NextNumber(-scale,scale),0)
            task.wait(.065)
        end
        if hum and hum.Parent then hum.CameraOffset=offset end
    end)
    soundAt(15,origin)
    -- Camera already shakes during revival; rumble on supported controllers.
    local gp=Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
    pcall(function()
        if HapticService:IsVibrationSupported(gp) and HapticService:IsMotorSupported(gp,Enum.VibrationMotor.Large) then
            HapticService:SetMotor(gp,Enum.VibrationMotor.Large,.85)
            task.delay(.8,function()pcall(function()HapticService:SetMotor(gp,Enum.VibrationMotor.Large,0)end)end)
        end
    end)
end
feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,p)
    if kind~="Visual" or type(p)~="table" or typeof(p.origin)~="Vector3" or not near(p.origin) then return end
    local mode=p.kind
    if mode=="BossRevival" then revive(p)
    elseif mode=="BossReviveLanding" then
        local color=typeof(p.color)=="Color3" and p.color or Color3.fromRGB(252,211,133)
        disc(p.origin+Vector3.new(0,2,0),90,color,.80)
        pillars(p.origin,color,18,55,30,1.0)
        burst(p.origin+Vector3.new(0,9,0),color,85,43,1.25)
        vibrate(p.origin,.98,.87)
        soundAt(15,p.origin)
    elseif mode=="BossAscended" then
        local col=typeof(p.color)=="Color3" and p.color or Color3.fromRGB(255,205,128)
        pillars(p.origin,col,14,48,26,1.4)
        disc(p.origin+Vector3.new(0,2,0),65,col,.9)
        burst(p.origin+Vector3.new(0,14,0),col,78,36,1.5)
        soundAt(6,p.origin)
        vibrate(p.origin,.8,.60)
    elseif mode=="BossPhaseStrike" or mode=="BossStrike" then
        onStrike(p)
        local step=tonumber(p.step) or 1
        vibrate(p.origin,step&gt;=6 and .72 or .52,step&gt;=11 and .42 or .26)
    end
end)

          <string name="Name">LashboundV22WhipPowerFX</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V22: stronger, animated elemental ability presence, no client-side damage.
-- Brief, pooled-by-lifetime primitives only. Each power has a distinct 3-D silhouette.
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RS=game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Tween=game:GetService("TweenService")
local Debris=game:GetService("Debris")
local player=Players.LocalPlayer
local Feedback=RS:WaitForChild("LashboundV5Remotes"):WaitForChild("Feedback")
local effects=Instance.new("Folder")
effects.Name="LashboundV22PowerVolume"
effects.Parent=workspace
local rng=Random.new()
local function near(p)
    local c=player.Character
    local root=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return root and (root.Position-p).Magnitude&lt;165
end
local function make(name,at,sz,clr,life,material,shape,alpha)
    local p=Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name=name;p.Size=sz;p.CFrame=CFrame.new(at)
    p.Material=material or Enum.Material.Neon
    p.Color=clr;p.Shape=shape or Enum.PartType.Block
    p.Transparency=alpha or .18
    p.Anchored=true;p.CanCollide=false;p.CanTouch=false;p.CanQuery=false
    p.CastShadow=false;p.Parent=effects
    Debris:AddItem(p,life+.13)
    return p
end
local function breath(p,color,scale,duration)
    local orb=make("ElementalBreath",p,Vector3.new(1.5,1.5,1.5),color,duration,Enum.Material.Glass,Enum.PartType.Ball,.58)
    Tween:Create(orb,TweenInfo.new(duration,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart),{
        Size=Vector3.new(scale,scale*.7,scale),Transparency=1}):Play()
end
local function strand(a,b,color,life,width)
    local delta=b-a
    if delta.Magnitude&lt;.1 then return end
    local p=make("ElementalStrand",(a+b)*.5,Vector3.new(width or .33,width or .33,delta.Magnitude),color,life)
    p.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)*.5,b)
    Tween:Create(p,TweenInfo.new(life),{Transparency=1}):Play()
end
local function circle(at,radius,col,life,height)
    local n=16
    local y=height or 1.2
    local prev=nil
    for i=0,n do
        local a=i*math.pi*2/n
        local q=at+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*radius,y,math.sin(a)*radius)
        if prev then strand(prev,q,col,life,.28) end
        prev=q
    end
end
local function dust(at,color,lifetime,number)
    local anchor=make("AuraParticleEmitter",at,Vector3.new(.1,.1,.1),color,lifetime,Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,Enum.PartType.Block,1)
    local em=Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    em.Texture="rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
    em.Lifetime=NumberRange.new(.25,lifetime)
    em.Rate=0
    em.Speed=NumberRange.new(6,18)
    em.SpreadAngle=Vector2.new(140,140)
    em.Drag=6
    em.LightEmission=.8
    em.Color=ColorSequence.new(color,Color3.new(1,1,1))
    em.Size=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,1.4),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0)})
    em.Parent=anchor
    em:Emit(number)
end
-- V26 universal 3D skill impact family: vertical energy columns, raised
-- shockwave perimeter, inner flash sphere and additive particles. All cosmetic;
-- the authoritative hitboxes and status effects remain on the server.
local function skillVolume(p,shock)
    local col=typeof(p.color)=="Color3" and p.color or Color3.fromRGB(240,211,116)
    local dir=typeof(p.direction)=="Vector3" and p.direction or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
    dir=Vector3.new(dir.X,0,dir.Z)
    if dir.Magnitude&lt;.05 then dir=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) else dir=dir.Unit end
    local origin=p.origin+(shock and Vector3.zero or dir*9)
    local radius=shock and 7.5 or 9
    local life=shock and .34 or .52
    for i=0,11 do
        local a=2*math.pi*i/12
        local q=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*radius,1,math.sin(a)*radius)
        local nextA=2*math.pi*(i+1)/12
        local nextQ=origin+Vector3.new(math.cos(nextA)*radius,1,math.sin(nextA)*radius)
        strand(q,nextQ,col,life,.38)
        if i%3==0 then
            local ht=shock and 7 or 12
            local pillar=make("SkillEnergyPillar",q+Vector3.new(0,ht*.5,0),Vector3.new(.85,ht,.85),col,life,Enum.Material.Neon,Enum.PartType.Cylinder,.38)
            Tween:Create(pillar,TweenInfo.new(life,Enum.EasingStyle.Quad),{Transparency=1,Size=Vector3.new(.18,ht*1.12,.18)}):Play()
        end
    end
    local flash=make("SkillExpandingCore",origin+Vector3.new(0,3.3,0),Vector3.new(1.8,1.8,1.8),col,life,Enum.Material.Glass,Enum.PartType.Ball,.45)
    Tween:Create(flash,TweenInfo.new(life,Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Size=Vector3.new(radius*1.55,radius*.86,radius*1.55),Transparency=1}):Play()
    dust(origin+Vector3.new(0,3,0),col,life,shock and 8 or 15)
end

local last={}
local function power(p)
    local point=p.origin
    local id=tostring(p.whipId or "Rope")
    local clr=typeof(p.color)=="Color3" and p.color or Color3.fromRGB(200,216,255)
    local direction=typeof(p.direction)=="Vector3" and p.direction or Vector3.new(0,0,-1)
    local dir=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)
    if dir.Magnitude&lt;.05 then dir=Vector3.new(0,0,-1) else dir=dir.Unit end
    local right=Vector3.new(-dir.Z,0,dir.X)
    local center=point+dir*13
    dust(point+Vector3.new(0,2,0),clr,.65,10)
    if id=="Rope" then
        for k=-1,1 do strand(point+Vector3.new(k*.9,3,0),point+dir*30+Vector3.new(k*1.5,2,0),clr,.42,.37) end
        breath(point+dir*24,clr,7,.43)
    elseif id=="Ember" then
        for i=1,3 do
            task.delay(.11*(i-1),function()
                if effects.Parent then
                    circle(point,8+i*4,clr,.4,1+i*.3)
                    dust(point+Vector3.new(0,1,0),clr,.6,8)
                end
            end)
        end
        breath(point+Vector3.new(0,4,0),Color3.fromRGB(255,117,45),17,.6)
    elseif id=="Frost" then
        for k=-4,4 do
            local origin=center+right*k*2.4
            strand(origin+Vector3.new(0,1,0),origin+Vector3.new(0,12-k%3,0),Color3.fromRGB(218,247,255),.65,.65)
        end
        breath(center,clr,19,.7)
    elseif id=="Volt" or id=="Judgement" then
        for k=-3,3 do
            local q=point+dir*(14+k*2)+right*(k*2.4)
            strand(q+Vector3.new(0,19,0),q+Vector3.new(0,1,0),Color3.fromRGB(255,240,141),.3,.39)
            dust(q+Vector3.new(0,2,0),clr,.38,5)
        end
        breath(center,clr,13,.45)
    elseif id=="Gravity" then
        circle(center,13,clr,.85,1)
        circle(center,8,Color3.fromRGB(221,194,255),.85,4)
        breath(center+Vector3.new(0,5,0),clr,15,.9)
    elseif id=="Echo" then
        circle(point,11,clr,.6,1.7)
        for i=1,7 do
            local a=i*math.pi*2/7
            local orbit=point+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*7,2,math.sin(a)*7)
            strand(point+Vector3.new(0,2,0),orbit,clr,.43,.32)
            breath(orbit,clr,3.8,.48)
        end
        breath(point+Vector3.new(0,3,0),clr,11,.75)
    elseif id=="Phantom" then
        for k=-2,2 do
            strand(point+right*k*1.4+Vector3.new(0,2,0),point+dir*25+right*k*2.5+Vector3.new(0,3.5,0),clr,.48,.3)
        end
        breath(point+dir*25,clr,11,.5)
    elseif id=="Legend" then
        circle(point+dir*20,10,clr,.76,1.5)
        for i=0,7 do
            local a=2*math.pi*i/8
            local start=point+dir*20+Vector3.new(math.cos(a)*9,1,math.sin(a)*9)
            strand(start,start+Vector3.new(0,13,0),clr,.55,.31)
        end
        breath(point+dir*20,clr,13,.6)
    elseif id=="Owner" then
        circle(point,16,clr,.65,1.4)
        breath(point+dir*22,clr,20,.55)
    end
end
Feedback.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(kind,_,p)
    if kind~="Visual" or type(p)~="table" or typeof(p.origin)~="Vector3" then return end
    if p.kind~="Power" and p.kind~="PowerPulse" then return end
    if not near(p.origin) then return end
    local id=tostring(p.whipId or "Rope")
    local actor=tostring(p.actorUserId or 0)
    local now=os.clock()
    local key=actor..id
    if now-(last[key] or -20)&lt;.22 then return end
    last[key]=now
    if p.kind=="Power" then power(p) end
    skillVolume(p,p.kind=="PowerPulse")
end)

      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX05051">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundV25EnemyMotion</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- LASHBOUND V25: distance-budgeted full-body Sentinel procedural animation.
-- 12 enemy style variations x 8 movement/action states x 16 poses = 1,536
-- baked procedural pose samples, blended smoothly. These are NOT 1,536
-- uploaded Animation assets or 1,536 animation tracks running together.
-- Server alone owns NPC combat, navigation, hitboxes, health and AI decisions.
local Workspace=game:GetService("Workspace")
local Players=game:GetService("Players")
local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
local player=Players.LocalPlayer
local renderables=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local registering=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local STYLES=12
local FRAMES=16
local MODES={"Idle","Walk","Strafe","Attack","Dodge","Stun","Flinch","Recover"}
local BONE_NAMES={
    root="RootJoint",neck="Neck",armR="Right Shoulder",armL="Left Shoulder",
    hipR="Right Hip",hipL="Left Hip",elbowR="Right Elbow",elbowL="Left Elbow",
    wristR="Right Wrist",wristL="Left Wrist",kneeR="Right Knee",kneeL="Left Knee",
    ankleR="Right Ankle",ankleL="Left Ankle",spine="SpineFlex",
    grip="BotWhipGrip",
}
local function ease(t) t=math.clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t) end
local function cf(p,y,r) return CFrame.Angles(p or 0,y or 0,r or 0) end
local function makePose(style,mode,t)
    local phase=t*math.pi*2
    local gait=math.sin(phase)
    local body=math.sin(phase*2+.2*style)
    local styleVar=1+(style%4)*.045
    local side=(style%2==0) and -1 or 1
    local stride=0;local bend=0;local attack=0;local flinch=0;local dodge=0
    local crouch=0;local twist=0;local head=0
    if mode=="Idle" then
        stride=.025; crouch=.018*body; head=math.sin(phase*.55+style)*.05
    elseif mode=="Walk" then
        stride=.78; crouch=.075*math.abs(body);twist=.082*gait
    elseif mode=="Strafe" then
        stride=.52; crouch=.085*math.abs(body);twist=.15*side
    elseif mode=="Attack" then
        attack=math.sin(math.pi*ease(t))
        local release=ease((t-.58)/.20)
        twist=side*(.40*attack-.68*release)
        crouch=-.17*attack+.06*release
        stride=.1
        bend=release*.40
    elseif mode=="Dodge" then
        dodge=math.sin(math.pi*t)
        twist=side*.36*dodge
        crouch=-.18*dodge
        stride=.32*dodge
    elseif mode=="Stun" then
        crouch=-.22;head=-.17+.08*body;twist=.12*body
    elseif mode=="Flinch" then
        flinch=math.sin(math.pi*ease(t))
        crouch=-.12*flinch;twist=-side*.33*flinch;head=.2*flinch
    else
        bend=.08*math.sin(math.pi*t)
        head=.07*body
    end
    local legR=stride*gait*styleVar
    local legL=-legR
    local armR=-legR*.38
    local armL=-legL*.42
    if mode=="Attack" then
        local raise=ease(t/.36)
        local snap=ease((t-.58)/.17)
        local recover=ease((t-.82)/.18)
        armR=-1.20*raise+2.1*snap-1.0*recover
        armL=-.26*raise+.18*snap
    elseif mode=="Flinch" then
        armR=-.42*flinch;armL=-.35*flinch
    elseif mode=="Dodge" then
        armR=-.32*dodge;armL=-.28*dodge
    end
    local elbowR=(mode=="Attack" and (.78*attack+.23*bend)) or .12+.1*math.abs(gait)
    local elbowL=(mode=="Attack" and (.35*attack)) or .09+.1*math.abs(gait)
    return {
        root=CFrame.new(0,crouch,0)*cf(.065*body+flinch*.17,twist,.11*side*dodge),
        neck=cf(head,-twist*.30,.045*body),
        spine=cf(-.06*attack+.04*body,twist*.23,0),
        armR=cf(armR,twist*.35,side*(.16*attack+.10*dodge)),
        armL=cf(armL,-twist*.32,-side*(.12*attack+.1*dodge)),
        elbowR=cf(-elbowR,side*.08*attack,0),elbowL=cf(-elbowL,0,0),
        wristR=cf(.20*attack+.07*gait,0,-side*.32*attack),wristL=cf(.02+.1*flinch,0,0),
        hipR=cf(legR+.22*dodge,0,.045*body),hipL=cf(legL+.22*dodge,0,-.045*body),
        kneeR=cf(-.18-math.max(0,legR)*.75-.45*dodge,0,0),
        kneeL=cf(-.18-math.max(0,legL)*.75-.45*dodge,0,0),
        ankleR=cf(.06+math.max(0,legR)*.24,0,0),ankleL=cf(.06+math.max(0,legL)*.24,0,0),
        grip=cf(.12*attack+.05*body,0,side*.07*attack),
    }
end
local library={}
local keyCount=0
for style=1,STYLES do
    library[style]={}
    for _,mode in ipairs(MODES) do
        local samples={}
        for frame=1,FRAMES do
            samples[frame]=makePose(style,mode,(frame-1)/(FRAMES-1))
            keyCount+=1
        end
        library[style][mode]=samples
    end
end
assert(keyCount==1536,"Sentinel animation library must contain exactly 1536 pose samples")
local function sample(style,mode,t)
    local poses=library[style][mode]
    local v=math.clamp(t,0,1)*(FRAMES-1)
    local idx=math.floor(v)+1
    local a,b=poses[idx],poses[math.min(idx+1,FRAMES)]
    local alpha=ease(v-math.floor(v))
    local out={}
    for bone,transform in pairs(a) do out[bone]=transform:Lerp(b[bone],alpha) end
    return out
end
local function register(model)
    if renderables[model] or not model:IsA("Model") or model:GetAttribute("LashboundNPC")~=true
        or model:GetAttribute("LashboundDog") then return end
    local root=model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local humanoid=model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not humanoid or not model:FindFirstChild("Torso") then return end
    local joints={}
    for k,name in pairs(BONE_NAMES) do
        local m=model:FindFirstChild(name,true)
        if not m or not m:IsA("Motor6D") then return end
        joints[k]=m
    end
    local style=(tonumber(model.Name:match("_(%d+)$")) or 1)%STYLES+1
    renderables[model]={root=root,hum=humanoid,joints=joints,style=style,phase=style*.63,
        lastMode="Idle",current={},lastUpdate=0,lastLoc=0,walkBlend=0,attackProgress=0}
end
local function retry(model)
    if renderables[model] or registering[model] then return end
    registering[model]=true
    task.spawn(function()
        for _=1,36 do
            if not model.Parent or renderables[model] then break end
            register(model)
            if renderables[model] then break end
            task.wait(.15)
        end
        registering[model]=nil
    end)
end
for _,m in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
    if m:IsA("Model") and m:GetAttribute("LashboundNPC") and not m:GetAttribute("LashboundDog") then retry(m) end
end
Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(m)
    if m:IsA("Model") and m:GetAttribute("LashboundNPC") and not m:GetAttribute("LashboundDog") then retry(m) end
end)
local playerRoot=nil
local accumulate=0
RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(dt)
    dt=math.min(dt,.075)
    accumulate+=dt
    if accumulate&lt;1/30 then return end
    local tick=accumulate;accumulate=0
    local ch=player.Character
    playerRoot=ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not playerRoot then return end
    local now=Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
    for model,st in pairs(renderables) do
        if not model.Parent or st.hum.Health&lt;=0 or not st.root.Parent then
            renderables[model]=nil
        else
            local distance=(playerRoot.Position-st.root.Position).Magnitude
            local budget=distance&lt;95 and (1/30) or distance&lt;175 and .09 or .24
            if distance&lt;260 and now-st.lastUpdate&gt;=budget then
                local elapsed=st.lastUpdate&gt;0 and math.clamp(now-st.lastUpdate,.015,.15) or tick
                st.lastUpdate=now
                local v=st.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local speed=Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z).Magnitude
                local goal=math.clamp(speed/10,0,1)
                st.walkBlend+= (goal-st.walkBlend)*(1-math.exp(-elapsed*9))
                st.phase+=elapsed*(6.5+math.min(speed,20)*.34)
                local attackTime=now-(model:GetAttribute("BotAttackStart") or -100)
                local impactAt=model:GetAttribute("BotAttackImpact") or 0
                local dodgeAt=model:GetAttribute("BotDodgeStarted") or -100
                local flinchAt=model:GetAttribute("BotFlinchAt") or -100
                local mode="Idle";local t=(st.phase/(math.pi*2))%1
                if now-flinchAt&lt;.28 then mode="Flinch";t=(now-flinchAt)/.28
                elseif now-dodgeAt&lt;.52 then mode="Dodge";t=(now-dodgeAt)/.52
                elseif attackTime&gt;=0 and now&lt;impactAt+.38 then
                    mode="Attack";t=attackTime/math.max(.1,impactAt-(model:GetAttribute("BotAttackStart") or now)+.38)
                elseif st.hum.Health&lt;st.hum.MaxHealth*.32 and speed&gt;1.8 then mode="Strafe"
                elseif st.walkBlend&gt;.14 then mode=speed&gt;4.5 and "Walk" or "Strafe"
                elseif model:GetAttribute("BotFrozen") then mode="Stun" end
                local pose=sample(st.style,mode,t)
                local timeAlpha=1-math.exp(-elapsed*16)
                if mode~=st.lastMode then timeAlpha=math.min(timeAlpha,.35) end
                st.lastMode=mode
                for name,m in pairs(st.joints) do
                    if m.Parent then
                        local target=pose[name] or CFrame.identity
                        -- Transform is purely cosmetic: joints keep their welded,
                        -- server-authoritative character assembly and hitbox.
                        local from=st.current[name] or CFrame.identity
                        local blended=from:Lerp(target,timeAlpha)
                        st.current[name]=blended
                        m.Transform=blended
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
</ProtectedString>
        </Properties>
      </Item>
      <Item class="LocalScript" referent="RBX_LASHBOUND_V31_CAMERA">
        <Properties>
          <string name="Name">LashboundV31CameraToggle</string>
          <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
          <ProtectedString name="Source">-- Lashbound V31 | Toggle first/third person camera (V on keyboard, button on touch)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local firstPerson = false
local thirdMin = 8
local thirdMax = 22

local function applyCamera()
    if firstPerson then
        player.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson
    else
        player.CameraMode = Enum.CameraMode.Classic
        player.CameraMinZoomDistance = thirdMin
        player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = thirdMax
    end
end

local function toggleCamera()
    if not firstPerson then
        thirdMin = math.max(0.5, player.CameraMinZoomDistance)
        thirdMax = math.max(thirdMin, player.CameraMaxZoomDistance)
    end
    firstPerson = not firstPerson
    applyCamera()
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.V or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR3 then toggleCamera() end
end)

do -- Always show the camera toggle on PC, console, and touch.
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "LashboundCameraToggleGui"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Name = "CameraToggle"
    button.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 42)
    button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -305)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 28, 38)
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    button.TextScaled = true
    button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    button.Text = "CAMERA: 3P"
    button.Parent = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    button.Selectable = true
    button.ZIndex = 12
    -- Stay above attack, power, and dodge even on compact/touch viewports.
    local function place()
        local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local viewport=camera.ViewportSize
        button.Size=viewport.Y&lt;560 and UDim2.fromOffset(94,34) or UDim2.fromOffset(112,42)
        button.Position=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,viewport.Y&lt;560 and -245 or -305)
    end
    place()
    local function watchCamera()
        local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal('ViewportSize'):Connect(place)
            place()
        end
    end
    workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal('CurrentCamera'):Connect(watchCamera)
    watchCamera()
    button.Activated:Connect(function()
        toggleCamera()
        button.Text = firstPerson and "CAMERA: 1P" or "CAMERA: 3P"
    end)
end

player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.defer(applyCamera)
end)
</ProtectedString>
        </Properties>
      </Item>
    </Item>
  </Item>
</roblox>
